# Adversarial correctness AUDIT — v15_m01_equity_reconstruct (the foundation of the selection engine)

Context: HL copy-trading. The research pipeline is: S3 ingest+taker-gate FEEDS m1-m10 (the selection engine).
m01 = `research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py` reconstructs each wallet's EQUITY / POSITION timeline from
raw fills + marks. It is the FOUNDATION: every gate above it (journeys, folds, eligibility, ranking, survival,
gates) depends on m01 being correct. The owner does NOT trust m1-m10 and believes they are buggy. Your job:
find the bugs. Be maximally adversarial and concrete. Read the file fully.

## Hunt specifically for (report each with file:line + a CONCRETE failure case: inputs -> wrong output)
1. LOOK-AHEAD / future leakage: using a mark/price/fill from AFTER the timestamp being computed; asof joins that
   pick the NEXT bar instead of the last; equity at time t using data > t.
2. SURVIVORSHIP / selection-on-future: filtering the wallet universe or coins by future-known activity.
3. POSITION/EQUITY RECONSTRUCTION errors: sign errors (buy/sell, long/short), cost-basis/VWAP mistakes on
   scale-ins/scale-outs/reversals, funding/fee not applied or double-applied, realized-vs-unrealized confusion,
   closedPnl misuse, cross-dex (main/xyz/flx) mixing, position not netting to zero when it should.
3b. MARK correctness: staleness cap wrong, oracle-vs-mark-vs-mid confusion, coin not found -> silent 0 or NaN,
   mark at fill-ts+latency vs fill-ts.
4. FEE/SLIPPAGE coin-blindness: hardcoded 8.64bps or fee_rt() without coin= (undercharges HIP-3/xyz `:` coins).
5. SILENT DATA LOSS: bare except: pass, dropna that silently removes rows, fillna(0) that fabricates equity,
   groupby losing rows, int/float truncation, tz/ms-vs-s timestamp bugs, off-by-one on window/fold boundaries.
6. CARRY-IN truncation: windowing fills so a position open before the window start fabricates a fake entry.
7. STREAMING/MEMORY: does it build one giant in-RAM frame (violates the mandatory streaming-IO rule)?
8. Any other correctness bug that would make the reconstructed equity/positions WRONG.

## Output
A PRIORITIZED bug catalog: P0 (makes equity/positions wrong -> poisons every downstream gate) / P1 / P2, each
with file:line, the concrete failure case, and a one-line fix direction. If a section is clean, say so. End with
an OVERALL trust verdict for m01: can its output be trusted as-is, or is it poisoned until the P0/P1s are fixed?

     8	def _fill(ts, side, size, start, tid):
     9	    return {
    10	        "coin": "BTC", "side": side, "size": float(size), "price": 100.0,
    11	        "time": ts, "tid": tid, "startPosition": float(start),
    12	        "signed_sz": float(size) if side == "B" else -float(size),
    13	        "fee": 0.1, "builderFee": 0.0, "deployerFee": 0.0,
    14	    }

    17	def test_burst_action_uses_position_chain_without_equity():
    18	    fills = [
    19	        _fill(1000, "A", 855, -573, 10),
    20	        _fill(1000, "A", 573, -3011, 20),
    21	        _fill(1000, "A", 1583, -1428, 30),
    22	    ]
    23	    rows = ei.build_burst_actions("0xABC", fills)
    24	    assert len(rows) == 1
    25	    row = rows[0]
    26	    assert row["action_type"] == "ADDON"
    27	    assert row["position_before"] == -573.0
    28	    assert row["position_after"] == -3584.0
    29	    assert row["n_source_fills"] == 3
    30	    assert row["copy_signal_valid"] is True
    31	    assert row["live_open_candidate"] is False
    32	
    33	
    34	def test_cross_burst_gap_resync_is_explicit_but_current_signal_valid():

    36	    rows = ei.build_burst_actions("0xabc", fills)

    42	    # raw-trade tracker could not repair the missing state: fail closed.

    44	    assert rows[1]["copy_signal_valid"] is False

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""V15 M01 — Whole-account Hyperliquid PERP equity reconstructor.
     3	
     4	This is the V15 foundation module. It reconstructs each source wallet's
     5	WHOLE-ACCOUNT perp equity (summed across every markable perp dex), re-anchored
     6	to HL's true weekly ``portfolio.perpAllTime`` accountValue, and decomposes the
     7	result into a flow-neutral trading-ROE plus per-wallet accuracy diagnostics.
     8	
     9	It is a NEW file: it does NOT modify any running strategy. It adapts the proven
    10	re-anchoring forward walk and ledger-cash-delta logic from
    11	``scripts/v13_equity_reconstruct_v8.py`` but corrects the two structural flaws
    12	of that module for V15:
    13	
    14	  (A) WHOLE-ACCOUNT instead of MAIN-only. perpAllTime is the SUM of per-dex
    15	      clearinghouseState marginSummary.accountValue across every perp dex
    16	      (verified live 2026-05-30 on 0x2fcb6898: main 591818 + xyz 2357394 =
    17	      2949212 = last perpAllTime point). v8 reconstructed MAIN only while
    18	      anchoring to a whole-account number -> drift for multi-dex wallets. Here
    19	      we reconstruct ALL perp dexes (main + every "<dex>:" prefix incl xyz/cash/
    20	      hyna/para/vntl/flx/km), value positions with per-coin marks, and only
    21	      EXCLUDE spot (@-tokens, USDC). Unmarkable coins are flagged (residual
    22	      notional) and gate the day, not silently dropped.
    23	
    24	  (B) FLOW-NEUTRAL ROE. We emit a cumulative external-capital flow series
    25	      (``ext_flow_cum``) so downstream modules can compute
    26	      ``trading_pnl = equity_change - ext_flow`` and a flow-neutral return.
    27	
    28	CORE MODEL (verified, brain projects/quant/v15/equity-model-verified):
    29	    equity = cash + Sum_coin signed_pos * mark_at(t)
    30	    perpAllTime(t) = sum over perp dexes of marginSummary.accountValue
    31	    (collateral + uPnL, perp-only; spot + staking EXCLUDED).
    32	
    33	METHOD (re-anchored forward walk, adapted from v8 compute_eq_at):
    34	    For each weekly perpAllTime anchor (t_a, eq_a):
    35	      seed pre-anchor positions; snap cash := eq_a - Sum pos*mark(t_a);
    36	      walk the merged fill/ledger/funding event stream in (t_a, t]:
    37	        fill   -> cash += -signed_sz*px - fee;
    38	                  pos[coin] += signed_sz
    39	        ledger -> cash += taxonomy_cash_delta; ext_flow_cum += neutralize_delta
    40	        funding-> cash += usdc
    41	      equity(t) = cash + Sum pos*mark(t).
    42	    Emit an EOD series (one row per UTC day), using the latest weekly anchor
    43	    at-or-before EOD. Days with any missing mark are flagged, not silently
    44	    corrupted.
    45	
    46	DATA SOURCES (all LOCAL except the API anchor + today cross-check):
    47	  - Anchor truth (weekly): HL API portfolio.perpAllTime accountValueHistory.
    48	  - Today cross-check: HL API clearinghouseState per dex (marginSummary +
    49	    assetPositions).
    50	  - Anchor positions seed: app/data/v13/wallet_anchor_state.parquet.
    51	  - Fills: app/data/hl_s3_fills_v2/YYYYMMDD.parquet (full enriched schema).
    52	  - Funding: app/data/v13/raw_funding_cache_20k/{wallet}_*.json (S3 bulk).
    53	  - Ledger: app/data/v13/raw_ledger_cache_20k/{wallet}_*.json.
    54	  - Marks: Mongo hyperliquid_candles (1m, s3_reconstructed); close at-or-before
    55	    ts. Candles END 2026-05-23 13:55 UTC -> window end clamped to 2026-05-23.
    56	
    57	OUTPUTS (parquet, one row per wallet-DAY):
    58	    wallet, date, equity_usd, cash, position_value_usd,
    59	    gross_position_notional_usd, n_positions,
    60	    ext_flow_cum (true series cumulative), segment_ext_flow (per-anchor segment),
    61	    n_unmarkable_positions, unmarkable_notional_usd, anchor_age_h,
    62	    return_since_anchor_pct, recon_incomplete, has_liquidation_in_day,
    63	    source_dexes
    64	  Plus a per-wallet ``.audit.parquet``:
    65	    wallet, n_fills, n_funding, n_ledger, n_weekly_anchors_in_window,
    66	    max_inter_anchor_drift_pct, median_inter_anchor_drift_pct,
    67	    n_segments_reconciled, max_segment_reconcile_err_usd, today_crosscheck_pct,
    68	    n_position_mismatches, unknown_ledger_types, quarantined, ...
    69	
    70	CLI:
    71	    python v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py --wallets-file W.txt \
    72	        --start 2025-12-01 --end 2026-05-23 --output out.parquet [--validate]
    73	
    74	The ``--validate`` mode prints a per-wallet accuracy table (inter-anchor drift,
    75	segment realized-PnL reconcile error, today cross-check, position mismatches)
    76	and does NOT require writing the full series.
    77	"""

   189	# Per-coin in-memory 1m close series for ASOF lookups (replaces per-action Mongo round-trips, which
   190	# were ~94% of M2 wall time: 1.3M find_one calls / 10 wallets). Each coin loaded ONCE (one indexed
   191	# range query) into sorted (minutes, closes) numpy arrays; get_mark then does a binary-search asof.
   192	# Bounded memory: only coins actually touched, one series each. (Perf decision 2026-05-31.)
   193	_mark_series: dict[str, tuple] = {}

   202	
   203	def get_mark(coin: str, ts_ms: int, causal: bool = False) -> Optional[float]:
   204	    """1m candle close at-or-before ts_ms (latest). None if no candle covers it.
   205	
   206	    Works for any prefix (main + xyz:/cash:/hyna:/para:/vntl:/flx:/km:) since the
   207	    candle ``coin`` carries the full prefixed symbol.
   208	
   209	    EXACT-MARK FIX (2026-06-04, Alberto "reconcile to the cent"): for EXOTIC HIP-3
   210	    coins (prefixed dexes xyz:/cash:/flx:/vntl:/km:/para:/hyna:) the trade-candle close
   211	    diverges materially from HL's setOracle mark (observed xyz:SILVER -12.8%, xyz:GOLD
   212	    -4.7% on 2026-03-04) -> on leveraged positions this is a dominant equity-recon drift
   213	    source. HL computes accountValue on the MARK (oracle), not the last trade. So we now
   214	    PREFER the oracle mark (hyperliquid_oracle, from v13_extract_oracle_marks setOracle)
   215	    for prefixed coins, with a candle fallback when oracle is absent/stale. Main-dex
   216	    (unprefixed) coins keep the trade-candle close (their oracle is consensus-level, not
   217	    in explorer_blocks; main mark precision tracked separately via asset_ctxs).
   218	
   219	    Set causal=True for forward decisions: use the last one-minute bar whose
   220	    close time is <= ts_ms, matching M07's convention (bar_open <= ts - 60s).
   221	    Keep causal=False for point-in-time MTM where the mark genuinely existed at ts_ms.
   222	    """
   223	    lookup_ms = (ts_ms // 60_000) * 60_000 - 60_000 if causal else ts_ms
   224	    # EXACT MARK first: exotic (prefixed) coins -> setOracle oracle; MAIN (unprefixed) coins ->
   225	    # asset_ctxs mark_px (HL's exact per-minute mark, the price accountValue uses). Candle fallback.
   226	    if ":" in coin:
   227	        # HL values accountValue/uPnL at the MARK price (markPxs[0]); ORACLE px is FUNDING-ONLY
   228	        # and is WRONG for m2m (robust-price-indices doc; Alberto, repeatedly). markPx ONLY here --
   229	        # NO oracle in the m2m path. If markPx is absent, fall through to the trade-candle last
   230	        # resort (still imperfect on thin books, but never the funding oracle).
   231	        mkpx = _markpx_lookup(coin, lookup_ms, ORACLE_MAX_AGE_MS)
   232	        if mkpx is not None:
   233	            return mkpx
   234	    else:
   235	        mpx = _assetctx_lookup(coin, lookup_ms, ASSETCTX_MAX_AGE_MS)
   236	        if mpx is not None:
   237	            return mpx
   238	    minute_key = lookup_ms // 60_000 * 60_000
   239	    mins, closes = _coin_series(coin)
   240	    if mins.size == 0:
   241	        return None
   242	    i = int(np.searchsorted(mins, minute_key, side="right")) - 1  # latest candle minute <= minute_key
   243	    if i < 0:
   244	        return None
   245	    # STALENESS CAP (codex marks-gate 2026-06-24): the candle fallback is at-or-before with NO age
   246	    # bound, so a candle from hours ago was returned as if current -> silently-stale MTM that callers
   247	    # treat as a live mark (markable_all=True). Cap the as-of age: if the nearest prior 1m bar is older
   248	    # than CANDLE_MAX_AGE_MS, return None so the caller flags it (degraded / NO_ANCHOR) instead of
   249	    # marking on a stale price. With dense all-market candles this rarely triggers; it only catches the
   250	    # genuinely-dormant-coin case the cap is meant to surface.
   251	    if minute_key - int(mins[i]) > CANDLE_MAX_AGE_MS:
   252	        return None
   253	    px = closes[i]
   254	    return None if px != px else float(px)  # NaN-safe (missing close -> None)
   255	
   256	
   257	# Candle-fallback staleness cap (codex marks-gate 2026-06-24). The 1m candle close is an at-or-before
   258	# lookup; without a bound a stale bar (delisted/dormant coin, or a data gap) is returned as a live mark.
   259	# Set to 15min to MATCH ASSETCTX_MAX_AGE_MS (codex recheck 2026-06-24: prefer 15min for a deploy-driving
   260	# reconstruction; 60min still allows materially-stale MTM on a fast-moving thin coin). With dense
   261	# all-market 1m candles a held coin has a bar most minutes, so this fires only on genuine dormancy/gaps
   262	# -> caller flags degraded/NO_ANCHOR. The pre/post-05-24 coverage+staleness AUDIT validates the cap does
   263	# not over-flag thin HIP-3 instruments; bump back toward 60min only if the audit shows false positives.
   264	CANDLE_MAX_AGE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000  # 15min (matches ASSETCTX_MAX_AGE_MS)
   265	
   266	# Equity-dependent selection requires repeated out-of-sample anchor checks.
   267	# One or zero segments is not enough evidence that the reconstruction is
   268	# stable; such wallets remain usable only in the equity-independent lane.
   269	MIN_INTER_ANCHOR_CHECKS = 2
   270	
   271	
   272	# Oracle/setOracle series, per-coin in-memory asof cache. ``mark_px`` is the
   273	# exact account-value mark when observed, but a sparse historical extraction is
   274	# not permission to reuse it for hours. The 30-wallet multi-dex sensitivity
   275	# showed 15 minutes weakly dominated 6 hours on inter-anchor drift (6 improved,
   276	# 0 worsened for median drift; 7 improved, 0 worsened for max drift). Older
   277	# observations therefore fall back to the contemporaneous trade candle.
   278	_oracle_series: dict[str, tuple] = {}
   279	ORACLE_MAX_AGE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000
   280	
   281	
   282	def _load_oracle_from_mongo(coin: str) -> tuple:
   283	    db = get_mongo()
   284	    cur = db.hyperliquid_oracle.find(
   285	        {"coin": coin}, projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "oracle_px": 1, "_id": 0}
   286	    )
   287	    pts: list[tuple[int, float]] = []
   288	    for d in cur:
   289	        t = d.get("timestamp_utc")
   290	        p = d.get("oracle_px")
   291	        if t is None or p is None:
   292	            continue
   293	        try:
   294	            pts.append((int(t), float(p)))
   295	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   296	            continue
   297	    pts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
   298	    if not pts:
   299	        return (np.empty(0, dtype="int64"), np.empty(0, dtype="float64"))
   300	    return (np.asarray([x[0] for x in pts], dtype="int64"),
   301	            np.asarray([x[1] for x in pts], dtype="float64"))
   302	
   303	
   304	def _oracle_lookup(coin: str, ts_ms: int, max_age_ms: int) -> Optional[float]:
   305	    s = _oracle_series.get(coin)
   306	    if s is None:
   307	        s = _load_oracle_from_mongo(coin)
   308	        _oracle_series[coin] = s
   309	    ts_arr, px = s
   310	    if ts_arr.size == 0:
   311	        return None
   312	    i = int(np.searchsorted(ts_arr, ts_ms, side="right")) - 1  # latest oracle point <= ts_ms
   313	    if i < 0:
   314	        return None
   315	    if ts_ms - int(ts_arr[i]) > max_age_ms:  # too stale -> let caller fall back to candle
   316	        return None
   317	    v = px[i]
   318	    return None if v != v else float(v)
   319	
   320	
   321	# setOracle MARK price (markPxs[0]) -- the price HL uses for accountValue/uPnL (oracle is funding-only).
   322	# Mirrors _oracle_lookup; reads the mark_px field written by scripts/pull_oracle_targeted.py.
   323	_markpx_series: dict[str, tuple] = {}
   324	
   325	
   326	def _load_markpx_from_mongo(coin: str) -> tuple:
   327	    db = get_mongo()
   328	    cur = db.hyperliquid_oracle.find(
   329	        {"coin": coin, "mark_px": {"$ne": None}},
   330	        projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "mark_px": 1, "_id": 0},
   331	    )
   332	    pts: list[tuple[int, float]] = []
   333	    for d in cur:
   334	        t = d.get("timestamp_utc")
   335	        p = d.get("mark_px")
   336	        if t is None or p is None:
   337	            continue
   338	        try:
   339	            pts.append((int(t), float(p)))
   340	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   341	            continue
   342	    pts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
   343	    if not pts:
   344	        return (np.empty(0, dtype="int64"), np.empty(0, dtype="float64"))
   345	    return (np.asarray([x[0] for x in pts], dtype="int64"),
   346	            np.asarray([x[1] for x in pts], dtype="float64"))
   347	
   348	
   349	def _markpx_lookup(coin: str, ts_ms: int, max_age_ms: int) -> Optional[float]:
   350	    s = _markpx_series.get(coin)
   351	    if s is None:
   352	        s = _load_markpx_from_mongo(coin)
   353	        _markpx_series[coin] = s
   354	    ts_arr, px = s
   355	    if ts_arr.size == 0:
   356	        return None
   357	    i = int(np.searchsorted(ts_arr, ts_ms, side="right")) - 1
   358	    if i < 0:
   359	        return None
   360	    if ts_ms - int(ts_arr[i]) > max_age_ms:
   361	        return None
   362	    v = px[i]
   363	    return None if v != v else float(v)
   364	
   365	
   366	# asset_ctxs EXACT mark for MAIN coins: per-coin (minute_ms, mark_px) .npy written by
   367	# scripts/extract_asset_ctx_marks.py. 1-min granularity -> tight staleness cap. Mirrors _coin_series.
   368	_assetctx_series: dict[str, tuple] = {}
   369	ASSETCTX_MAX_AGE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000  # 15min (asset_ctxs is 1-min; small cap)
   370	ASSETCTX_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "app" / "data" / "v15" / "assetctx_marks"
   371	
   372	
   373	def _assetctx_lookup(coin: str, ts_ms: int, max_age_ms: int) -> Optional[float]:
   374	    s = _assetctx_series.get(coin)
   375	    if s is None:
   376	        p = ASSETCTX_DIR / f"{_ulib.quote(coin, safe='')}.npy"
   377	        if p.exists():
   378	            try:
   379	                arr = np.load(p, mmap_mode="r")
   380	                s = (np.asarray(arr[0], dtype="int64"), np.asarray(arr[1], dtype="float64"))
   381	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
   382	                s = (np.empty(0, dtype="int64"), np.empty(0, dtype="float64"))
   383	        else:
   384	            s = (np.empty(0, dtype="int64"), np.empty(0, dtype="float64"))
   385	        _assetctx_series[coin] = s
   386	    ts_arr, px = s
   387	    if ts_arr.size == 0:
   388	        return None
   389	    i = int(np.searchsorted(ts_arr, ts_ms, side="right")) - 1
   390	    if i < 0:
   391	        return None
   392	    if ts_ms - int(ts_arr[i]) > max_age_ms:
   393	        return None
   394	    v = px[i]
   395	    return None if v != v else float(v)
   396	
   397	
   398	# Earliest ms at which a coin has ANY price (candle minute, else oracle ts). A coin cannot be
   399	# held before it has a price (its listing) -> used to bound phantom position back-projection in
   400	# seed_positions. None = no price source ever. Cached.
   401	_coin_first_price: dict[str, Optional[int]] = {}
   402	
   403	
   404	def _coin_first_price_ms(coin: str) -> Optional[int]:
   405	    if coin in _coin_first_price:
   406	        return _coin_first_price[coin]
   407	    fp: Optional[int] = None
   408	    mins, _ = _coin_series(coin)
   409	    if mins.size:
   410	        fp = int(mins[0])
   411	    if fp is None:  # no candle -> try oracle series
   412	        oser = _oracle_series.get(coin)
   413	        if oser is None:
   414	            oser = _load_oracle_from_mongo(coin)
   415	            _oracle_series[coin] = oser
   416	        ts_arr, _ = oser
   417	        if ts_arr.size:
   418	            fp = int(ts_arr[0])
   419	    _coin_first_price[coin] = fp
   420	    return fp
   421	
   422	
   423	import urllib.parse as _ulib
   424	
   425	MARKS_CACHE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "app" / "data" / "v15" / "marks_cache"
   426	
   427	
   428	def _coin_cache_path(coin: str) -> Path:
   429	    return MARKS_CACHE_DIR / f"{_ulib.quote(coin, safe='')}.npy"
   430	
   431	
   432	def _load_coin_from_mongo(coin: str) -> tuple:
   433	    db = get_mongo()
   434	    cur = db.hyperliquid_candles.find(
   435	        {"coin": coin, "interval": "1m"},
   436	        projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "close": 1, "_id": 0},
   437	    ).sort("timestamp_utc", 1)
   438	    mins_l: list[int] = []
   439	    closes_l: list[float] = []
   440	    for d in cur:
   441	        t = d.get("timestamp_utc")
   442	        if t is None:
   443	            continue
   444	        c = d.get("close")
   445	        mins_l.append(int(t))
   446	        closes_l.append(float(c) if c is not None else float("nan"))
   447	    return (np.asarray(mins_l, dtype="int64"), np.asarray(closes_l, dtype="float64"))
   448	
   449	
   450	def _marks_manifest_path(out_dir: Path) -> Path:
   451	    return out_dir / "_manifest.json"
   452	
   453	
   454	def marks_cache_status(out_dir: Path | None = None) -> dict:
   455	    """Cheap freshness check: compares the cached coin-set against Mongo's current 1m coin-set + the
   456	    latest 1m candle minute (indexed). Returns {fresh, age_days, reason}. (codex perf-r1 #3)"""
   457	    import json as _json
   458	    out_dir = Path(out_dir) if out_dir else MARKS_CACHE_DIR
   459	    mp = _marks_manifest_path(out_dir)
   460	    if not mp.exists():
   461	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": None, "reason": "no manifest"}
   462	    man = _json.loads(mp.read_text())
   463	    db = get_mongo()
   464	    cur_coins = set(db.hyperliquid_candles.distinct("coin", {"interval": "1m"}))
   465	    # latest 1m candle minute overall (index-backed via the coin+interval+ts index is per-coin; use a
   466	    # bounded find sorted desc which the planner serves from the compound index).
   467	    latest = db.hyperliquid_candles.find_one({"interval": "1m"}, sort=[("timestamp_utc", -1)],
   468	                                             projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "_id": 0})
   469	    cur_max = int(latest["timestamp_utc"]) if latest else 0
   470	    cur_total = int(db.hyperliquid_candles.estimated_document_count())  # O(1) metadata; catches backfills
   471	    import time as _t
   472	    age_days = (_t.time() - man.get("built_unix", 0)) / 86400.0
   473	    if set(man.get("coins", [])) != cur_coins:
   474	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": age_days, "reason": f"coin set changed ({len(cur_coins)} now vs {len(man.get('coins', []))} cached)"}
   475	    if cur_max > int(man.get("max_minute", 0)):
   476	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": age_days, "reason": f"new candles since build (max {cur_max} > {man.get('max_minute')})"}
   477	    # codex perf-r2 #3: total-count signature catches INTERIOR backfills (gap fills / corrections that
   478	    # add docs) that do NOT advance the global max minute. (In-place value overwrites that preserve
   479	    # doc count remain undetectable cheaply -> use --rebuild-marks-cache after any such correction.)
   480	    if "total_candles" not in man:
   481	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": age_days, "reason": "manifest missing total_candles; rebuild required"}
   482	    if cur_total != int(man["total_candles"]):
   483	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": age_days, "reason": f"candle count changed ({cur_total} vs {man['total_candles']}) -> possible backfill"}
   484	    return {"fresh": True, "age_days": age_days, "reason": "coin-set + max-minute + count match"}
   485	
   486	
   487	def build_marks_cache(out_dir: Path | None = None, force: bool = False) -> int:
   488	    """PRECOMPUTE ONCE (single process, one Mongo pass per coin): write each coin's 1m (minute,close)
   489	    series to a local .npy so the worker pool reads marks from local files instead of each of N
   490	    workers re-scanning Mongo (the 74%-idle I/O stall). Returns #coins written. Idempotent: skips
   491	    coins already cached unless force. Writes a manifest for freshness validation. Unique temp names
   492	    so concurrent builds never clobber. (Perf decision 2026-05-31; mandatory streaming-io sibling.)"""
   493	    import json as _json
   494	    import time as _t
   495	    out_dir = Path(out_dir) if out_dir else MARKS_CACHE_DIR
   496	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   497	    db = get_mongo()
   498	    coins = db.hyperliquid_candles.distinct("coin", {"interval": "1m"})
   499	    n = 0
   500	    max_minute = 0
   501	    for coin in coins:
   502	        p = out_dir / f"{_ulib.quote(coin, safe='')}.npy"
   503	        if p.exists() and not force:
   504	            continue
   505	        mins, closes = _load_coin_from_mongo(coin)
   506	        if mins.size:
   507	            max_minute = max(max_minute, int(mins[-1]))
   508	        arr = np.vstack([mins.astype("float64"), closes]) if mins.size else np.empty((2, 0), dtype="float64")
   509	        # codex perf-r1 #4: unique tmp (pid) so concurrent builds/runs never race on one tmp name.
   510	        tmp = p.with_name(f"{p.name}.{os.getpid()}.tmp")
   511	        with open(tmp, "wb") as fh:   # file handle: np.save must NOT re-append .npy to the name
   512	            np.save(fh, arr)
   513	        tmp.replace(p)
   514	        n += 1
   515	    # manifest: record the full coin set + the latest candle minute observed (freshness signature).
   516	    latest = db.hyperliquid_candles.find_one({"interval": "1m"}, sort=[("timestamp_utc", -1)],
   517	                                             projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "_id": 0})
   518	    cur_max = int(latest["timestamp_utc"]) if latest else max_minute
   519	    _marks_manifest_path(out_dir).write_text(_json.dumps({
   520	        "built_unix": _t.time(), "coins": list(coins), "n_coins": len(coins), "max_minute": cur_max,
   521	        "total_candles": int(db.hyperliquid_candles.estimated_document_count()),
   522	    }))
   523	    logger.info(f"build_marks_cache: wrote {n} coin series to {out_dir} ({len(coins)} coins total)")
   524	    return n
   525	
   526	
   527	def _coin_series(coin: str) -> tuple:
   528	    """Lazily load+cache a coin's FULL 1m (minute, close) series as sorted numpy arrays. Prefers the
   529	    local precomputed .npy (page-cached, shared across workers, NO Mongo); falls back to a one-shot
   530	    Mongo range query if the cache file is absent. Equivalent to the prior per-call
   531	    `timestamp_utc <= minute_key, sort desc, limit 1` lookup (proven byte-identical in validation)."""
   532	    s = _mark_series.get(coin)
   533	    if s is not None:
   534	        return s
   535	    p = _coin_cache_path(coin)
   536	    if p.exists():
   537	        try:
   538	            arr = np.load(p, mmap_mode="r")
   539	            s = (np.asarray(arr[0], dtype="int64"), np.asarray(arr[1], dtype="float64"))
   540	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001  corrupt cache -> Mongo fallback
   541	            s = _load_coin_from_mongo(coin)
   542	    else:
   543	        s = _load_coin_from_mongo(coin)
   544	    _mark_series[coin] = s
   545	    return s
   546

   548	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   549	# Coin / dex helpers
   550	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   551	
   552	
   553	def coin_is_spot(coin: str) -> bool:
   554	    """Return true for non-perp trade assets excluded from reconstruction.
   555	
   556	    ``@`` and slash-delimited symbols denote spot pairs; ``#`` denotes HIP-4
   557	    outcome markets. Outcome fills have Buy/Sell/Settlement semantics, not perp
   558	    position semantics.
   559	    """
   560	    return coin.startswith(("@", "#")) or "/" in coin or coin == "USDC"
   561	
   562	
   563	def coin_is_dropped(coin: str) -> bool:
   564	    """Return whether a configured perp-dex prefix is excluded."""
   565	    return any(coin.startswith(p) for p in DROPPED_DEX_PREFIXES)
   566	
   567	
   568	def coin_dex(coin: str) -> str:
   569	    """Return the perp dex name for a coin. Unprefixed -> 'main'."""
   570	    if ":" in coin:
   571	        return coin.split(":", 1)[0]
   572	    return "main"
   573	
   574	
   575	# ALL DEXES IN SCOPE (Alberto 2026-05-30: "ALL dexes become IN SCOPE").
   576	# The only equity history HL gives is whole-account perpAllTime (the portfolio
   577	# `dex` param is IGNORED -> verified 2026-05-30, it returns the same whole-account
   578	# number for dex=main/xyz/default). So reconstructing EVERY perp dex makes our
   579	# equity match the anchor instead of fighting it. Any perp coin (any "<dex>:"
   580	# prefix, incl flx:, or unprefixed main) is in scope; spot (@-tokens,
   581	# slash-delimited pairs, USDC) and HIP-4 outcome markets (#-tokens) are excluded.
   582	# (USDC capital-flow dex scoping is handled by dex_in_scope(): any non-spot dex.)
   583	# Dexes with a row in wallet_anchor_state.parquet (position seed source). main +
   584	# xyz + flx are seedable from the parquet; cash/hyna/para/vntl seed from fills
   585	# only (still IN SCOPE, just no static-anchor seed -> rely on fills + drift gate).
   586	ANCHOR_COVERED_DEXES = frozenset({"main", "xyz", "flx"})
   587	
   588	
   589	def coin_is_allowed_perp(coin: str) -> bool:
   590	    """True for any in-scope perp; excludes spot and HIP-4 outcomes."""
   591	    return not coin_is_spot(coin)
   592	
   593	
   594	def dex_in_scope(dex: str) -> bool:
   595	    """A dex name (coin_dex output / ledger sourceDex) is in scope if it is any
   596	    perp dex. Spot markers are out. All perp dexes are in scope."""
   597	    d = (dex or "").strip().lower()
   598	    return d not in ("spot", "@")
   599

   601	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   602	# HL API: weekly anchor + today cross-check
   603	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   604	
   605	
   606	PERP_ANCHOR_CACHE = Path(
   607	    "/Users/hermes/quants-lab/app/data/v15/perp_anchor_cache"
   608	)
   609	
   610	
   611	def get_portfolio_perp(wallet: str, retries: int = 4) -> list[tuple[int, float]]:
   612	    """perpAllTime accountValueHistory as (ts_ms, value), whole-account perp.
   613	
   614	    DISK-CACHED (2026-05-30): the per-wallet HL `portfolio` API call was the M01
   615	    bottleneck (~0.3-0.7s each, network-bound, 20k wallets sequential = hours).
   616	    We now persist each wallet's perpAllTime history to
   617	    app/data/v15/perp_anchor_cache/{wallet}.json on first fetch and read it back
   618	    on every subsequent run -> future M01 runs do ZERO API calls for anchors.
   619	    Raw data, never auto-deleted. Delete the dir to force a refresh.
   620	    """
   621	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
   622	    cache_fp = PERP_ANCHOR_CACHE / f"{wallet_lc}.json"
   623	    if cache_fp.exists():
   624	        try:
   625	            with open(cache_fp) as f:
   626	                return [(int(t), float(v)) for t, v in json.load(f)]
   627	        except Exception:
   628	            pass  # corrupt cache -> refetch
   629	
   630	    result: list[tuple[int, float]] = []
   631	    fetched = False
   632	    for i in range(retries):
   633	        try:
   634	            r = requests.post(
   635	                HL_URL, json={"type": "portfolio", "user": wallet}, timeout=20
   636	            )
   637	            if r.status_code == 429:
   638	                time.sleep(2**i)
   639	                continue
   640	            if r.status_code != 200:
   641	                fetched = False
   642	                break
   643	            for window_name, wd in r.json():
   644	                if window_name == "perpAllTime":
   645	                    result = [
   646	                        (int(x[0]), float(x[1]))
   647	                        for x in wd.get("accountValueHistory", [])
   648	                    ]
   649	                    break
   650	            fetched = True
   651	            break
   652	        except Exception:
   653	            time.sleep(1)
   654	
   655	    # Persist only a genuine API response (incl. legitimately-empty histories) so
   656	    # we never cache a transient network failure as "no anchors".
   657	    if fetched:
   658	        try:
   659	            PERP_ANCHOR_CACHE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   660	            tmp = cache_fp.with_suffix(".json.tmp")
   661	            with open(tmp, "w") as f:
   662	                json.dump([[int(t), float(v)] for t, v in result], f)
   663	            tmp.replace(cache_fp)
   664	        except Exception:
   665	            pass
   666	    return result
   667	
   668	
   669	WHOLE_ANCHOR_CACHE = Path(
   670	    "/Users/hermes/quants-lab/app/data/v15/whole_anchor_cache"
   671	)
   672	
   673	
   674	def get_portfolio_all(wallet: str, retries: int = 4) -> list[tuple[int, float]]:
   675	    """`allTime` WHOLE-ACCOUNT accountValueHistory as (ts_ms, value).
   676	
   677	    2026-06-08 (SOLVED -- spot inclusion): the perpAllTime anchor is PERP-ONLY, but many wallets hold
   678	    most of their equity in SPOT (e.g. 0x292ae77a: $10,941 total / $877 perp). Reconstructing perp-only
   679	    against a perp-only anchor while the spot side dominates produced the large inter-anchor drift. We now
   680	    reconstruct the WHOLE ACCOUNT (perp + spot, both marked) and anchor to HL's `allTime` series, which
   681	    sits in the SAME portfolio response next to perpAllTime. Cached separately so perpAllTime is preserved.
   682	    Raw data, never auto-deleted. Delete the dir to force a refresh.
   683	    """
   684	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
   685	    cache_fp = WHOLE_ANCHOR_CACHE / f"{wallet_lc}.json"
   686	    if cache_fp.exists():
   687	        try:
   688	            with open(cache_fp) as f:
   689	                return [(int(t), float(v)) for t, v in json.load(f)]
   690	        except Exception:
   691	            pass
   692	    result: list[tuple[int, float]] = []
   693	    fetched = False
   694	    for i in range(retries):
   695	        try:
   696	            r = requests.post(HL_URL, json={"type": "portfolio", "user": wallet}, timeout=20)
   697	            if r.status_code == 429:
   698	                time.sleep(2**i)
   699	                continue
   700	            if r.status_code != 200:
   701	                fetched = False
   702	                break
   703	            for window_name, wd in r.json():
   704	                if window_name == "allTime":
   705	                    result = [(int(x[0]), float(x[1])) for x in wd.get("accountValueHistory", [])]
   706	                    break
   707	            fetched = True
   708	            break
   709	        except Exception:
   710	            time.sleep(1)
   711	    if fetched:
   712	        try:
   713	            WHOLE_ANCHOR_CACHE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   714	            tmp = cache_fp.with_suffix(".json.tmp")
   715	            with open(tmp, "w") as f:
   716	                json.dump([[int(t), float(v)] for t, v in result], f)
   717	            tmp.replace(cache_fp)
   718	        except Exception:
   719	            pass
   720	    return result
   721	
   722	
   723	def get_clearinghouse(wallet: str, dex: Optional[str], retries: int = 3) -> Optional[dict]:
   724	    """Per-dex clearinghouseState snapshot (today cross-check). dex=None -> main."""
   725	    body: dict = {"type": "clearinghouseState", "user": wallet}
   726	    if dex and dex != "main":
   727	        body["dex"] = dex
   728	    for i in range(retries):
   729	        try:
   730	            r = requests.post(HL_URL, json=body, timeout=20)
   731	            if r.status_code == 429:
   732	                time.sleep(2**i)
   733	                continue
   734	            if r.status_code == 200:
   735	                return r.json()
   736	            return None
   737	        except Exception:
   738	            time.sleep(1)
   739	    return None
   740	
   741	
   742	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   743	# Local data loaders
   744	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   745	
   746	
   747	def _position_key(value: float) -> float:
   748	    """Stable key for decimal position values represented as float."""
   749	    return round(float(value), 10)
   750	
   751	
   752	def _order_fill_burst(group: list[dict]) -> tuple[list[dict], bool]:
   753	    """Order one same-wallet/coin/millisecond burst by position continuity.
   754	
   755	    ``tid`` identifies a trade but is not a causal sequence number. Each fill's
   756	    startPosition and signed size define an edge in the actual position path.
   757	    """
   758	    if len(group) <= 1:
   759	        return list(group), True
   760	    from collections import Counter
   761	
   762	    starts = [_position_key(f["startPosition"]) for f in group]
   763	    ends = [_position_key(f["startPosition"] + f["signed_sz"]) for f in group]
   764	    start_counts, end_counts = Counter(starts), Counter(ends)
   765	    path_starts = [k for k, n in start_counts.items() if n > end_counts.get(k, 0)]
   766	    if len(path_starts) == 1:
   767	        current = path_starts[0]
   768	    elif all(f["signed_sz"] > 0 for f in group):
   769	        current = min(starts)
   770	    elif all(f["signed_sz"] < 0 for f in group):
   771	        current = max(starts)
   772	    else:
   773	        current = min(starts, key=lambda x: (abs(x), x))
   774	
   775	    unused = set(range(len(group)))
   776	    ordered: list[dict] = []
   777	    complete = True
   778	    while unused:
   779	        candidates = [i for i in unused if starts[i] == current]
   780	        if not candidates:
   781	            complete = False
   782	            candidates = list(unused)
   783	        i = min(candidates, key=lambda j: (int(group[j].get("tid", 0) or 0), j))
   784	        ordered.append(group[i])
   785	        unused.remove(i)
   786	        current = ends[i]
   787	    return ordered, complete
   788	
   789	
   790	def order_wallet_fills_causally(fills: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   791	    """Causally order fills and attach a stable per-wallet ``fill_seq``."""
   792	    groups: dict[tuple[int, str], list[dict]] = {}
   793	    for f in fills:
   794	        groups.setdefault((int(f["time"]), str(f["coin"])), []).append(f)
   795	    out: list[dict] = []
   796	    for key in sorted(groups):
   797	        burst, complete = _order_fill_burst(groups[key])
   798	        for f in burst:
   799	            f["causal_order_ok"] = bool(complete)
   800	            out.append(f)
   801	    for seq, f in enumerate(out):
   802	        f["fill_seq"] = seq
   803	    return out
   804	
   805	
   806	def load_wallet_fills(wallet: str, t0: int, t1: int) -> list[dict]:
   807	    """Load ALL markable perp fills for one wallet in [t0, t1].
   808	
   809	    Includes every perp dex prefix except explicitly dropped prefixes. Spot
   810	    fills (@-prefix, USDC) are excluded: M1 is anchored to ``perpAllTime`` and
   811	    M7/live copy execution is perp-only.
   812	    Casts string numerics, computes signed_sz (+size if side=='B' else -size),
   813	    and tags liquidation fills via the `dir` column.
   814	
   815	    Fast path: per-wallet partitioned parquet if present; else scan dailies
   816	    filtering wallet (lowercased compare).
   817	    """
   818	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
   819	    cols = [
   820	        "wallet", "coin", "side", "size", "price", "time", "dir",
   821	        "closedPnl", "startPosition", "fee", "builderFee", "deployerFee",
   822	        "tid",
   823	    ]
   824	
   825	    def _normalize(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list[dict]:
   826	        out: list[dict] = []
   827	        for rec in df.to_dict("records"):
   828	            coin = str(rec["coin"])
   829	            if not coin_is_allowed_perp(coin):
   830	                continue
   831	            size = float(rec["size"])
   832	            try:
   833	                tid = int(rec.get("tid", 0) or 0)
   834	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
   835	                tid = 0
   836	            d = {
   837	                "coin": coin,
   838	                "side": rec["side"],
   839	                "size": size,
   840	                "price": float(rec["price"]),
   841	                "time": int(rec["time"]),
   842	                "tid": tid,
   843	                "dir": str(rec.get("dir", "") or ""),
   844	                "closedPnl": float(rec.get("closedPnl", 0) or 0),
   845	                "startPosition": float(rec.get("startPosition", 0) or 0),
   846	                "fee": float(rec.get("fee", 0) or 0),
   847	                "builderFee": float(rec.get("builderFee", 0) or 0),
   848	                "deployerFee": float(rec.get("deployerFee", 0) or 0),
   849	            }
   850	            d["signed_sz"] = size if rec["side"] == "B" else -size
   851	            d["is_liquidation"] = d["dir"] in LIQUIDATION_DIRS
   852	            d["is_spot"] = False
   853	            out.append(d)
   854	        return out
   855	
   856	    fills: list[dict] = []
   857	    by_wallet_path = S3_BY_WALLET_DIR / f"{wallet_lc}.parquet"
   858	    if by_wallet_path.exists():
   859	        try:
   860	            df = pd.read_parquet(by_wallet_path)
   861	            df = df[[c for c in cols if c in df.columns]].copy()
   862	            df["time"] = df["time"].astype("int64")
   863	            df = df[(df["time"] >= t0) & (df["time"] <= t1)]
   864	            return order_wallet_fills_causally(_normalize(df))
   865	        except Exception:
   866	            fills = []
   867	
   868	    for ff in sorted(glob.glob(str(S3_FILLS_DIR / "*.parquet"))):
   869	        try:
   870	            # Read only columns that actually exist (read_parquet with a fixed
   871	            # `columns=` list raises if ANY is absent, and the bare `except`
   872	            # below would then silently drop the WHOLE day of fills). Require
   873	            # the core accounting columns; tolerate missing optional fee cols.
   874	            avail = set(pd.ParquetFile(ff).schema_arrow.names)
   875	            required = {"wallet", "coin", "side", "size", "price", "time"}
   876	            missing_required = required - avail
   877	            if missing_required:
   878	                logger.warning(
   879	                    f"  skip {Path(ff).name}: missing required cols {sorted(missing_required)}"
   880	                )
   881	                continue
   882	            read_cols = [c for c in cols if c in avail]
   883	            df = pd.read_parquet(ff, columns=read_cols)
   884	            df["time"] = df["time"].astype("int64")
   885	            df["_w"] = df["wallet"].astype(str).str.lower()
   886	            m = df[(df["_w"] == wallet_lc) & (df["time"] >= t0) & (df["time"] <= t1)]
   887	            if not m.empty:
   888	                fills.extend(_normalize(m))
   889	        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
   890	            logger.warning(f"  skip {Path(ff).name}: {e!r}")
   891	            continue
   892	    return order_wallet_fills_causally(fills)
   893	
   894	
   895	def load_wallet_funding(wallet: str, t0: int, t1: int) -> list[dict]:
   896	    """Load + merge + dedup funding events from the local S3 bulk cache.
   897	
   898	    Multiple {wallet}_{start}_{end}.json files may exist; merge them and dedup
   899	    by (time, coin) since overlapping windows can repeat the same record.
   900	    Records: {time, hash, delta:{type:'funding', coin, usdc(signed), ...}}.
   901	    """
   902	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
   903	    seen: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()
   904	    out: list[dict] = []
   905	    for fp in sorted(glob.glob(str(FUNDING_DIR / f"{wallet_lc}_*.json"))):
   906	        try:
   907	            with open(fp) as f:
   908	                data = json.load(f)
   909	        except Exception:
   910	            continue
   911	        for rec in data:
   912	            t = int(rec.get("time", 0))
   913	            if t < t0 or t > t1:
   914	                continue
   915	            d = rec.get("delta", {})
   916	            if d.get("type") != "funding":
   917	                continue
   918	            coin = str(d.get("coin", ""))
   919	            key = (t, coin)
   920	            if key in seen:
   921	                continue
   922	            seen.add(key)
   923	            out.append(rec)
   924	    out.sort(key=lambda x: int(x["time"]))
   925	    return out
   926	
   927	
   928	def load_wallet_ledger(wallet: str, t0: int, t1: int) -> list[dict]:
   929	    """Load ledger entries from the local cache, dedup on the FULL delta payload.
   930	
   931	    Dedup key includes a canonical serialization of the whole delta (not just
   932	    its type), so two distinct deltas of the same type in one tx (same time +
   933	    hash) are NOT collapsed into one. Overlapping cache windows still dedup
   934	    identical records.
   935	    """
   936	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
   937	    seen: set[tuple] = set()
   938	    out: list[dict] = []
   939	    for lf in sorted(glob.glob(str(LEDGER_DIR / f"{wallet_lc}_*.json"))):
   940	        try:
   941	            with open(lf) as f:
   942	                data = json.load(f)
   943	        except Exception:
   944	            continue
   945	        for e in data:
   946	            t = int(e.get("time", 0))
   947	            if t < t0 or t > t1:
   948	                continue
   949	            d = e.get("delta", {})
   950	            # Canonical full-delta key: distinct same-type deltas in one tx differ
   951	            # in their payload (amount/coin/counterparty) and survive dedup.
   952	            key = (
   953	                t,
   954	                e.get("hash", ""),
   955	                json.dumps(d, sort_keys=True, default=str),
   956	            )
   957	            if key in seen:
   958	                continue
   959	            seen.add(key)
   960	            out.append(e)
   961	    out.sort(key=lambda x: int(x["time"]))
   962	    return out
   963	
   964	
   965	@dataclass
   966	class AnchorState:
   967	    """Per-wallet anchor snapshot aggregated across markable perp dexes."""
   968	
   969	    positions: dict[str, float]  # coin -> szi (markable dexes, flx dropped)
   970	    entry_px: dict[str, float]  # coin -> entryPx from anchor (notional proxy)
   971	    fetched_ms: int
   972	    dexes_seen: set[str]
   973	    has_flx_anchor: bool
   974	    has_unmarkable_dex_anchor: bool
   975	    # per-dex accountValue at fetch time (today cross-check reference)
   976	    acct_value_by_dex: dict[str, float]
   977	    aggregate_acct_value: float  # sum over parquet dex rows (main+xyz+flx)
   978	
   979	
   980	def load_wallet_anchor(wallet: str, anchor_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[AnchorState]:
   981	    """Build the seed AnchorState from wallet_anchor_state.parquet.
   982	
   983	    The parquet only carries main/xyz/flx rows. We seed positions from ALL three
   984	    (flx is IN SCOPE). Positions on other in-scope dexes (cash/hyna/para/vntl/km)
   985	    have no parquet row and seed from fills instead.
   986	    """
   987	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
   988	    wa = anchor_df[anchor_df["wallet"].str.lower() == wallet_lc]
   989	    if wa.empty:
   990	        return None
   991	    positions: dict[str, float] = {}
   992	    entry_px: dict[str, float] = {}
   993	    fetched_ms = 0
   994	    dexes_seen: set[str] = set()
   995	    acct_value_by_dex: dict[str, float] = {}
   996	    aggregate_acct_value = 0.0
   997	    has_flx_anchor = False
   998	    has_unmarkable_dex_anchor = False
   999	    for _, row in wa.iterrows():
  1000	        if not row["ok"]:
  1001	            continue
  1002	        dex = row["dex"]
  1003	        dexes_seen.add(dex)
  1004	        acct_val = float(row["accountValue"])
  1005	        acct_value_by_dex[dex] = acct_val
  1006	        ts_ms = int(float(row["fetched_at_ts"]) * 1000)
  1007	        fetched_ms = max(fetched_ms, ts_ms)
  1008	        if dex == "flx":
  1009	            # flx is now IN SCOPE (all dexes). Record presence AND seed its
  1010	            # positions from the anchor row (flx has a parquet row + marks).
  1011	            has_flx_anchor = acct_val > 1.0 or int(row["n_positions"]) > 0
  1012	        # An anchor row on a dex without a parquet seed source (outside main/xyz/
  1013	        # flx) is informational only now (all dexes in scope; such positions seed
  1014	        # from fills, not the parquet).
  1015	        if dex not in ANCHOR_COVERED_DEXES:
  1016	            if acct_val > 1.0 or int(row["n_positions"]) > 0:
  1017	                has_unmarkable_dex_anchor = True
  1018	        aggregate_acct_value += acct_val
  1019	        for p in json.loads(row["positions_json"]):
  1020	            coin = p["coin"]
  1021	            szi = float(p["szi"])
  1022	            if not coin_is_allowed_perp(coin):
  1023	                continue
  1024	            positions[coin] = szi
  1025	            try:
  1026	                entry_px[coin] = abs(float(p.get("entryPx", 0.0)))
  1027	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
  1028	                entry_px[coin] = 0.0
  1029	    return AnchorState(
  1030	        positions=positions,
  1031	        entry_px=entry_px,
  1032	        fetched_ms=fetched_ms,
  1033	        dexes_seen=dexes_seen,
  1034	        has_flx_anchor=bool(has_flx_anchor),
  1035	        has_unmarkable_dex_anchor=bool(has_unmarkable_dex_anchor),
  1036	        acct_value_by_dex=acct_value_by_dex,
  1037	        aggregate_acct_value=aggregate_acct_value,
  1038	    )
  1039	
  1040	
  1041	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1042	# Cash-delta functions
  1043	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1044	
  1045	
  1046	def fill_cash_delta(f: dict) -> float:
  1047	    """Per fill: ``-signed_sz * price - fee``.
  1048	
  1049	    Hyperliquid's API defines ``fee`` as the total wallet charge (signed;
  1050	    negative means maker rebate). ``builderFee`` and ``deployerFee`` are
  1051	    informational components of that total and must not be added again. Spot
  1052	    and dropped markets are excluded at load time, but guard defensively.
  1053	    """
  1054	    if not coin_is_allowed_perp(f["coin"]):
  1055	        return 0.0
  1056	    return -f["signed_sz"] * f["price"] - f["fee"]
  1057	
  1058	
  1059	@dataclass
  1060	class LedgerDelta:
  1061	    """Result of classifying a ledger event: cash impact + external-flow tag."""
  1062	
  1063	    cash: float  # delta to perp cash (affects equity walk)
  1064	    ext_flow: float  # portion to accumulate into ext_flow_cum (neutralizable)
  1065	    unknown: bool = False  # True if the type is unrecognized -> quarantine
  1066	
  1067	
  1068	def ledger_cash_delta(e: dict, wallet_lc: str) -> LedgerDelta:
  1069	    """Classify a ledger event into (cash_delta, ext_flow_delta, unknown).
  1070	
  1071	    Cash signs mirror v8's proven mapper. The NEW V15 behaviour is that every
  1072	    EXTERNAL-CAPITAL / non-copyable type ALSO reports its cash impact as
  1073	    ext_flow so downstream can neutralize it:
  1074	        trading_pnl = equity_change - ext_flow_cum.
  1075	    Trading results (fills, funding, liquidation) are NOT in ext_flow.
  1076	
  1077	    Unknown ledger types return unknown=True (caller quarantines the wallet);
  1078	    they are never silently zeroed.
  1079	    """
  1080	    d = e.get("delta", {})
  1081	    k = d.get("type", "")
  1082	
  1083	    def _f(*keys, default=0.0) -> float:
  1084	        for key in keys:
  1085	            v = d.get(key)
  1086	            if v is not None:
  1087	                return float(v)
  1088	        return float(default)
  1089	
  1090	    # --- External capital / non-copyable flows (cash == ext_flow) ----------- #
  1091	    if k == "deposit":
  1092	        c = _f("usdc")
  1093	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1094	    if k == "withdraw":
  1095	        c = -(_f("usdc") + _f("fee"))
  1096	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1097	    if k == "accountClassTransfer":
  1098	        # Perp-account model: spot -> perp is an inflow and perp -> spot an
  1099	        # outflow. Neutralize both as external/non-copyable capital flow.
  1100	        amt = abs(_f("usdc", "amount"))
  1101	        c = amt if bool(d.get("toPerp")) else -amt
  1102	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1103	    if k == "send":
  1104	        # Perp-account model: only a leg touching an in-scope perp dex changes
  1105	        # reconstructed perp cash. HIP-3 dexes can use non-USDC collateral
  1106	        # (e.g. USDH on flx/km, USDT0 on cash); ``usdcValue`` is the canonical
  1107	        # USD value for those sends. Spot-only token transfers remain zero
  1108	        # because neither leg is in scope. Self-transfers spot<->perp remain
  1109	        # perp inflows/outflows and are neutralized from trading ROE.
  1110	        user = (d.get("user") or "").lower()
  1111	        dest = (d.get("destination") or "").lower()
  1112	        src_dex = str(d.get("sourceDex", "")).strip().lower()
  1113	        dst_dex = str(d.get("destinationDex", "")).strip().lower()
  1114	        if d.get("usdcValue") is not None:
  1115	            amt = _f("usdcValue")
  1116	        elif d.get("token") == "USDC":
  1117	            amt = _f("amount")
  1118	        else:
  1119	            # A non-USDC perp transfer without a USD valuation cannot be
  1120	            # reconstructed safely. Quarantine rather than treating token
  1121	            # quantity as dollars.
  1122	            touches_perp = dex_in_scope(src_dex) or dex_in_scope(dst_dex)
  1123	            return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0, unknown=touches_perp)
  1124	        fee = _f("fee")
  1125	        c = 0.0
  1126	        if user == wallet_lc and dex_in_scope(src_dex):
  1127	            c -= amt + fee
  1128	        if dest == wallet_lc and dex_in_scope(dst_dex):
  1129	            c += amt
  1130	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1131	    if k == "spotTransfer":
  1132	        # Spot-side balance movement; accountClassTransfer/send carry any
  1133	        # corresponding movement into or out of perp cash.
  1134	        return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0)
  1135	    if k == "internalTransfer":
  1136	        if d.get("token") not in (None, "USDC"):
  1137	            return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0)
  1138	        user = (d.get("user") or "").lower()
  1139	        dest = (d.get("destination") or "").lower()
  1140	        amt = _f("usdc", "amount")
  1141	        fee = _f("fee")
  1142	        c = 0.0
  1143	        if user == wallet_lc and dest != wallet_lc:
  1144	            c = -(amt + fee)
  1145	        elif dest == wallet_lc and user != wallet_lc:
  1146	            c = amt
  1147	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1148	    if k == "subAccountTransfer":
  1149	        user = (d.get("user") or "").lower()
  1150	        dest = (d.get("destination") or "").lower()
  1151	        amt = _f("usdc", "amount")
  1152	        c = 0.0
  1153	        if user == wallet_lc:
  1154	            c = -amt
  1155	        elif dest == wallet_lc:
  1156	            c = amt
  1157	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1158	    if k == "vaultDeposit":
  1159	        c = -_f("usdc", "amount")
  1160	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1161	    if k == "vaultWithdraw":
  1162	        c = _f("netWithdrawnUsd", "usdc", "amount")
  1163	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1164	    if k == "vaultCreate":
  1165	        c = -(_f("usdc", "amount") + _f("fee"))
  1166	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1167	    if k in ("vaultDistribution", "vaultLeaderCommission"):
  1168	        c = _f("usdc", "amount")
  1169	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1170	    if k == "rewardsClaim":
  1171	        if d.get("token") != "USDC":
  1172	            return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0)
  1173	        c = _f("usdc", "amount")
  1174	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1175	    if k == "borrowLend":
  1176	        if d.get("token") != "USDC":
  1177	            return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0)
  1178	        amt = abs(_f("usdc", "amount"))
  1179	        op = (d.get("operation") or "").lower()
  1180	        if op in ("supply", "lend", "deposit"):
  1181	            return LedgerDelta(-amt, -amt)
  1182	        if op in ("withdraw", "redeem"):
  1183	            return LedgerDelta(amt, amt)
  1184	        # borrow / repay -> debt offsets cash, no equity change, no ext flow.
  1185	        return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0)
  1186	    if k == "activateDexAbstraction":
  1187	        ev_dex = str(d.get("dex", "")).strip().lower()
  1188	        if not dex_in_scope(ev_dex):  # all perp dexes in scope (was main-only)
  1189	            return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0)
  1190	        token = d.get("token")
  1191	        if token == "USDC" or token is None:
  1192	            c = -abs(_f("usdc", "amount"))
  1193	        else:
  1194	            c = -_f("fee")
  1195	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1196	    if k in ("accountActivationGas", "deployGasAuction", "gossipPriorityGasAuction"):
  1197	        # Operational cost; neutralize as external (non-trading).
  1198	        c = -abs(_f("usdc", "amount", "fee"))
  1199	        return LedgerDelta(c, c)
  1200	
  1201	    # --- Zero / ignore (no perp-equity impact) ------------------------------ #
  1202	    if k in ZERO_LEDGER_TYPES:
  1203	        return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0)
  1204	
  1205	    # --- Unknown type -> quarantine ----------------------------------------- #
  1206	    if k:
  1207	        return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0, unknown=True)
  1208	    return LedgerDelta(0.0, 0.0)
  1209	
  1210	
  1211	def funding_cash_delta(e: dict) -> float:
  1212	    """Funding USDC (signed, wallet perspective) for all included perp dexes.
  1213	    """
  1214	    d = e.get("delta", {})
  1215	    if d.get("type") != "funding":
  1216	        return 0.0
  1217	    coin = str(d.get("coin", ""))
  1218	    # Only funding on an included perp coin counts (parity with fills/positions).
  1219	    if not coin_is_allowed_perp(coin):
  1220	        return 0.0
  1221	    return float(d.get("usdc", 0))
  1222	
  1223	
  1224	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1225	# Position seeding + reconstruction
  1226	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1227	
  1228	
  1229	def positions_at(fills: list[dict], t_ms: int) -> dict[str, float]:
  1230	    """Position per coin at time t = (EARLIEST fill's startPosition) + cumulative
  1231	    sum of all signed sizes at-or-before t.
  1232	
  1233	    CRITICAL (root-cause of large drift, fixed 2026-05-30): do NOT use the LAST
  1234	    fill's startPosition + signed_sz. Large orders fill as a SAME-MILLISECOND
  1235	    BURST against many makers, and the S3 by-wallet partition carries no `tid`,
  1236	    so same-ms fills are in arbitrary order. Picking the "last" one grabs a
  1237	    mid-burst startPosition and invents a phantom open position (observed: a flat
  1238	    wallet reconstructed as -90,492 MERL because the last-by-order fill in a
  1239	    closing burst had startPosition=-105,010). The cumulative sum from the
  1240	    EARLIEST fill's startPosition is ORDER-INDEPENDENT and exact regardless of
  1241	    intra-ms ordering or a missing tid. (fills are time-sorted on load.)"""
  1242	    by_coin: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
  1243	    for f in fills:
  1244	        if f["time"] > t_ms:
  1245	            break
  1246	        by_coin.setdefault(f["coin"], []).append(f)
  1247	    positions: dict[str, float] = {}
  1248	    for coin, fs in by_coin.items():
  1249	        pos = fs[0]["startPosition"] + sum(x["signed_sz"] for x in fs)
  1250	        if abs(pos) > 1e-9:
  1251	            positions[coin] = pos
  1252	    return positions
  1253	
  1254	
  1255	@dataclass
  1256	class WalkResult:
  1257	    cash: float
  1258	    positions: dict[str, float]
  1259	    equity: float
  1260	    position_value: float  # independent mark-derived sum
  1261	    gross_position_notional: float  # sum(abs(szi * mark)); never net long/short legs
  1262	    n_unmarkable: int
  1263	    unmarkable_notional: float  # |szi * entry-ish proxy| for coins lacking a mark
  1264	    ext_flow_cum: float  # ext flow within (anchor_ms, t_ms] (per-SEGMENT, not series)
  1265	    had_unknown_ledger: bool
  1266	    anchor_unmarkable: int = 0  # seeded positions with NO mark at anchor_ms
  1267	
  1268	    @property
  1269	    def recon_incomplete(self) -> bool:
  1270	        """True if equity for this walk is NOT trustworthy: a seeded position
  1271	        lacked an anchor mark (corrupted cash snap) or a terminal mark was missing
  1272	        -> we cannot value the book, so the day must not be trusted. (Hidden
  1273	        no-anchor-dex exposure is caught at the wallet level by the inter-anchor
  1274	        DRIFT gate, not here, since by definition we cannot see it per-row.)"""
  1275	        return self.anchor_unmarkable > 0 or self.n_unmarkable > 0
  1276	
  1277	
  1278	def seed_positions(
  1279	    fills: list[dict],
  1280	    anchor: AnchorState,
  1281	    anchor_ms: int,
  1282	    causal_cutoff: bool = False,
  1283	) -> dict[str, float]:
  1284	    """Seed positions held at anchor_ms.
  1285	
  1286	    TWO paths:
  1287	    * causal_cutoff=True (per-EVENT walk): fully causal -- positions_at(fills<=anchor)
  1288	      + the anchor's OWN near-fetch snapshot. MUST NOT use any fill with time>anchor_ms
  1289	      (look-ahead). Unchanged.
  1290	    * causal_cutoff=False (daily reconstruction + cent reconciliation): EX-POST
  1291	      BURST-AWARE seed. pos = P0 + cumsum(signed_sz of fills<=anchor), where P0 (the
  1292	      pre-first-fill carry) is taken from the clean earliest-fill startPosition, EXCEPT
  1293	      when that earliest fill is a same-millisecond BURST (no tid -> arbitrary order ->
  1294	      mid-burst garbage startPosition), in which case P0 is anchored to the authoritative
  1295	      fetch-time snapshot: P0 = snapshot_position - sum(ALL signed_sz). This kills the
  1296	      phantom-seed leak (e2af8f54's 83-fill same-ms first DOGE fill read startPosition
  1297	      1.79M -> 1.79M DOGE seeded at a Dec anchor where the wallet held zero). Cumulative
  1298	      signed_sz is order-independent so only P0 needed the fix. Coins in the snapshot but
  1299	      never traded in-window (Case A) are seeded at the snapshot size at every anchor.
  1300	
  1301	    NOTE: this CHANGES the daily reconstruction output (it was wrong: phantom positions).
  1302	    Re-run M2->M10 on the corrected equity. The per-event causal path is byte-unchanged.
  1303	    """
  1304	    near_fetch = abs(anchor_ms - anchor.fetched_ms) <= 86_400_000
  1305	
  1306	    if causal_cutoff:
  1307	        # FULLY-CAUSAL seed for the per-event walk (codex r2 BUG): the seed serves
  1308	        # every event k in this anchor's segment and MUST NOT depend on any fill with
  1309	        # time > anchor_ms (look-ahead). Causal seed = positions from fills <= anchor_ms
  1310	        # + the anchor's OWN near-fetch snapshot, full stop. A far-from-fetch snapshot is
  1311	        # not causal evidence; any genuine unseen exposure surfaces as inter-anchor DRIFT.
  1312	        start_positions = positions_at(fills, anchor_ms)
  1313	        if near_fetch:
  1314	            for coin, szi in anchor.positions.items():
  1315	                if coin not in start_positions and abs(szi) >= 1e-9:
  1316	                    start_positions[coin] = float(szi)
  1317	        return start_positions
  1318	
  1319	    # ----- EX-POST BURST-AWARE seed (causal_cutoff=False; daily + reconciliation) ----- #
  1320	    # pos(anchor) = P0 + cumulative signed_sz of fills at-or-before the anchor. The
  1321	    # cumulative sum is order-independent and robust. P0 (pre-first-fill carry) was the
  1322	    # leak: M1 took it from the earliest fill's startPosition, but large orders fill as
  1323	    # SAME-MILLISECOND bursts and the S3 by-wallet partition carries no tid -> the fills
  1324	    # are in arbitrary order, so a burst startPosition is mid-burst GARBAGE. Observed
  1325	    # (Alberto "reconcile to the cent", 2026-06-04): e2af8f54's first DOGE fill is an
  1326	    # 83-fill same-ms burst reading startPosition 1,792,138 -> the old seed put 1.79M DOGE
  1327	    # at a December anchor where the wallet held ZERO (phantom; its mark drift == the
  1328	    # inter-anchor residual to the dollar). Same for SOL/kPEPE/MON. FIX: when the earliest
  1329	    # fill is a same-ms burst, take P0 from the AUTHORITATIVE fetch-time snapshot instead:
  1330	    # P0 = snapshot_position(coin) - sum(ALL signed_sz) (order-independent, ground-truth-
  1331	    # anchored). reconstruct_wallet loads fills through fetched_ms so the sum reaches the
  1332	    # snapshot. A CLEAN earliest fill keeps the forward startPosition (the snapshot carries
  1333	    # minor fetch-instant noise that would otherwise leak into every anchor, e.g. the
  1334	    # main-only wallet a9881f6f: backward-everywhere regressed it from $0.04 to $272 median,
  1335	    # burst-aware preserves $0.04). Validated: a9881f6f $0.04 max $2.10 PRESERVED; e2af8f54
  1336	    # median $18,628 -> $2.28; daf50e49 $4,787 -> $710; 4ac21adc $9,311 -> $99.
  1337	    by_coin: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
  1338	    for f in fills:
  1339	        by_coin.setdefault(f["coin"], []).append(f)  # already time-sorted on load
  1340	    cur = anchor.positions
  1341	    start_positions = {}
  1342	    for coin, fs in by_coin.items():
  1343	        if not coin_is_allowed_perp(coin):
  1344	            continue
  1345	        t0 = fs[0]["time"]
  1346	        burst = sum(1 for f in fs if f["time"] == t0) > 1
  1347	        net_all = 0.0
  1348	        cum_le = 0.0
  1349	        for f in fs:
  1350	            net_all += f["signed_sz"]
  1351	            if f["time"] <= anchor_ms:
  1352	                cum_le += f["signed_sz"]
  1353	        if burst:
  1354	            p0 = float(cur.get(coin, 0.0)) - net_all  # backward: perp snapshot-anchored
  1355	        else:
  1356	            p0 = float(fs[0]["startPosition"])         # forward: clean earliest fill
  1357	        pos = p0 + cum_le
  1358	        if abs(pos) > 1e-9:
  1359	            start_positions[coin] = pos
  1360	    # Case A: coins in the fetch snapshot but NEVER traded in-window -> established
  1361	    # before the window and held constant (no trades = no size change) -> seed the
  1362	    # snapshot size at every anchor.
  1363	    for coin, szi in cur.items():
  1364	        if coin not in by_coin and coin_is_allowed_perp(coin) and abs(szi) >= 1e-9:
  1365	            start_positions[coin] = float(szi)
  1366	    return start_positions
  1367	
  1368	
  1369	def compute_eq_at(
  1370	    stream: list[tuple[int, str, dict]],
  1371	    fills: list[dict],
  1372	    anchor: AnchorState,
  1373	    wallet_lc: str,
  1374	    t_ms: int,
  1375	    anchor_ms: int,
  1376	    anchor_eq: float,
  1377	    causal_seed: bool = False,
  1378	) -> WalkResult:
  1379	    """Re-anchored forward walk from anchor_ms to t_ms.
  1380	
  1381	    cash := anchor_eq - Sum pos*mark(anchor_ms); then walk the merged event
  1382	    stream in (anchor_ms, t_ms]. equity(t) = cash + Sum pos*mark(t_ms).
  1383	
  1384	    Missing marks are COUNTED (n_unmarkable) and their notional estimated, but
  1385	    not silently zeroed into equity; the caller decides whether to flag the day.
  1386	    """
  1387	    start_positions = seed_positions(fills, anchor, anchor_ms, causal_cutoff=causal_seed)
  1388	
  1389	    # Snap cash to the anchor equity using marked position value at anchor_ms.
  1390	    # If a SEEDED position has no mark at anchor_ms, the cash snap silently
  1391	    # absorbs its (unknown) value -> equity is corrupted. Count those so the
  1392	    # caller can flag/quarantine the day instead of trusting it (P0).
  1393	    anchor_pos_value = 0.0
  1394	    anchor_unmarkable = 0
  1395	    for c, sz in start_positions.items():
  1396	        if abs(sz) < 1e-9:
  1397	            continue
  1398	        mark = get_mark(c, anchor_ms)
  1399	        if mark is None and coin_is_spot(c):
  1400	            mark = _last_fill_price(fills, c, anchor_ms) or None  # spot: candle may be absent -> fill price
  1401	        if mark is not None:
  1402	            anchor_pos_value += sz * mark
  1403	        else:
  1404	            anchor_unmarkable += 1
  1405	    cash = anchor_eq - anchor_pos_value
  1406	
  1407	    positions = dict(start_positions)
  1408	    # Coins present in the anchor seed are already folded into both position
  1409	    # value and the cash snap. A coin first observed AFTER the anchor can still
  1410	    # reveal a pre-anchor carry via its authoritative startPosition. In causal
  1411	    # mode we fold that carry only when its first fill becomes observable; this
  1412	    # mirrors compute_event_equity and avoids both future leakage and the old
  1413	    # error of treating a trim as a fresh short/open.
  1414	    carried_in = set(start_positions)
  1415	    ext_flow_cum = 0.0
  1416	    had_unknown = False
  1417	    for ts, typ, ev in stream:
  1418	        if ts <= anchor_ms or ts > t_ms:
  1419	            continue
  1420	        if typ == "fill":
  1421	            if not coin_is_allowed_perp(ev["coin"]):
  1422	                continue
  1423	            coin = ev["coin"]
  1424	            if causal_seed and coin not in carried_in:
  1425	                carried_in.add(coin)
  1426	                sp = float(ev.get("startPosition", 0.0) or 0.0)
  1427	                if abs(sp) > 1e-9:
  1428	                    positions[coin] = positions.get(coin, 0.0) + sp
  1429	                    anchor_mark = get_mark(coin, anchor_ms)
  1430	                    if anchor_mark is None:
  1431	                        # The anchor cash allocation for this newly revealed
  1432	                        # carry is unknowable. Flag the walk incomplete rather
  1433	                        # than silently absorbing it into cash.
  1434	                        anchor_unmarkable += 1
  1435	                    else:
  1436	                        cash -= sp * anchor_mark
  1437	            cash += fill_cash_delta(ev)
  1438	            positions[coin] = positions.get(coin, 0.0) + ev["signed_sz"]
  1439	            if abs(positions[coin]) < 1e-9:
  1440	                positions.pop(coin, None)
  1441	        elif typ == "ledger":
  1442	            ld = ledger_cash_delta(ev, wallet_lc)
  1443	            cash += ld.cash
  1444	            ext_flow_cum += ld.ext_flow
  1445	            had_unknown = had_unknown or ld.unknown
  1446	        elif typ == "funding":
  1447	            cash += funding_cash_delta(ev)
  1448	
  1449	    # Mark-to-market at t_ms (independent of cash).
  1450	    pos_value = 0.0
  1451	    gross_position_notional = 0.0
  1452	    n_unmarkable = 0
  1453	    unmarkable_notional = 0.0
  1454	    for c, sz in positions.items():
  1455	        if abs(sz) < 1e-9:
  1456	            continue  # dust; not a real position
  1457	        mark = get_mark(c, t_ms)
  1458	        if mark is None and coin_is_spot(c):
  1459	            mark = _last_fill_price(fills, c, t_ms) or None  # spot: candle may be absent -> fill price
  1460	        if mark is None:
  1461	            n_unmarkable += 1
  1462	            # Best-effort notional proxy: last in-window fill price for the coin;
  1463	            # fall back to the anchor parquet entryPx for statically-held coins
  1464	            # that were never traded in-window (and thus have no fill price).
  1465	            proxy_px = _last_fill_price(fills, c, t_ms)
  1466	            if proxy_px <= 0.0:
  1467	                proxy_px = anchor.entry_px.get(c, 0.0)
  1468	            unmarkable_notional += abs(sz) * proxy_px
  1469	            continue
  1470	        pos_value += sz * mark
  1471	        gross_position_notional += abs(sz * mark)
  1472	    equity = cash + pos_value
  1473	    return WalkResult(
  1474	        cash=cash,
  1475	        positions=positions,
  1476	        equity=equity,
  1477	        position_value=pos_value,
  1478	        gross_position_notional=gross_position_notional,
  1479	        n_unmarkable=n_unmarkable,
  1480	        unmarkable_notional=unmarkable_notional,
  1481	        anchor_unmarkable=anchor_unmarkable,
  1482	        ext_flow_cum=ext_flow_cum,
  1483	        had_unknown_ledger=had_unknown,
  1484	    )
  1485	
  1486	
  1487	def _last_fill_price(fills: list[dict], coin: str, t_ms: int) -> float:
  1488	    """Notional-proxy price for an unmarkable coin position.
  1489	
  1490	    Prefers the last traded price at-or-before t_ms. If the position was carried
  1491	    in from pre-window history (seeded via startPosition, no fill at-or-before
  1492	    t_ms), fall back to the FIRST fill price after t_ms so we still produce a
  1493	    sane notional estimate. 0.0 only if the coin never appears in the fills.
  1494	    """
  1495	    px_before = 0.0
  1496	    px_after = 0.0
  1497	    for f in fills:
  1498	        if f["coin"] != coin:
  1499	            continue
  1500	        if f["time"] <= t_ms:
  1501	            px_before = f["price"]
  1502	        elif px_after == 0.0:
  1503	            px_after = f["price"]
  1504	    return px_before if px_before > 0.0 else px_after
  1505	
  1506	
  1507	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1508	# Per-wallet reconstruction + accuracy diagnostics
  1509	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1510	
  1511	
  1512	def _liquidation_days(fills: list[dict]) -> set:
  1513	    """UTC dates on which a liquidation fill occurred."""
  1514	    days = set()
  1515	    for f in fills:
  1516	        if f.get("is_liquidation"):
  1517	            days.add(pd.Timestamp(f["time"], unit="ms", tz="UTC").floor("D").date())
  1518	    return days
  1519	
  1520	
  1521	def reconstruct_wallet(args: tuple) -> dict:
  1522	    """Reconstruct one wallet's whole-account perp equity EOD series + audit.
  1523	
  1524	    Returns a dict with either {'error': ...} or {'series': df, 'audit': {...}}.
  1525	    In validate mode (validation_only=True) the series is still built (needed
  1526	    for the accuracy table) but accuracy functions are computed and surfaced.
  1527	    """
  1528	    if len(args) == 5:
  1529	        wallet, anchor, start_ms, end_ms, validation_only = args
  1530	        causal_seed = True
  1531	    else:
  1532	        wallet, anchor, start_ms, end_ms, validation_only, causal_seed = args
  1533	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
  1534	
  1535	    if anchor is None:
  1536	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_anchor"}
  1537	
  1538	    # 1) Weekly anchor truth: all included perp dexes, excluding spot.
  1539	    avh = get_portfolio_perp(wallet)
  1540	    n_sentinel_zeros = sum(1 for _, v in avh if v == 0.0)
  1541	    valid_anchors = [(t, v) for t, v in avh if v > 0.01]
  1542	    if not valid_anchors:
  1543	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_valid_anchors"}
  1544	
  1545	    # Reconstruct (EMIT) only up to the anchor fetch time. But LOAD events through
  1546	    # the anchor fetch time so a coin opened in the gap between mark-coverage-end
  1547	    # (end_ms) and the later anchor fetch is NOT mis-classified as "never traded"
  1548	    # and silently backfilled into history (codex P0). The walk filters events by
  1549	    # t_ms <= walk_end_ms, so loading past walk_end_ms changes seed CLASSIFICATION
  1550	    # only, never the emitted equity.
  1551	    walk_end_ms = min(end_ms, anchor.fetched_ms)
  1552	    load_end_ms = max(walk_end_ms, int(anchor.fetched_ms))
  1553	
  1554	    # 2) Load local event sources (through fetch time; see note above).
  1555	    fills = load_wallet_fills(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  1556	    funding = load_wallet_funding(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  1557	    ledger = load_wallet_ledger(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  1558	
  1559	    stream: list[tuple[int, str, dict]] = []
  1560	    for f in fills:
  1561	        stream.append((f["time"], "fill", f))
  1562	    for e in ledger:
  1563	        stream.append((int(e["time"]), "ledger", e))
  1564	    for e in funding:
  1565	        stream.append((int(e["time"]), "funding", e))
  1566	    stream.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
  1567	
  1568	    # In-window weekly anchors (bound to >= start_ms so the walk never picks a
  1569	    # pre-start anchor and walk through unloaded events).
  1570	    window_anchors = [(t, v) for t, v in valid_anchors if start_ms <= t <= walk_end_ms]
  1571	
  1572	    liq_days = _liquidation_days(fills)
  1573	    source_dexes = sorted({coin_dex(f["coin"]) for f in fills} | {
  1574	        coin_dex(c) for c in anchor.positions
  1575	    })
  1576	    # Perp dexes the wallet touches that have NO row in the anchor parquet
  1577	    # (cash/hyna/para/vntl). All dexes are IN SCOPE and reconstructed from fills;
  1578	    # this is now an INFORMATIONAL flag (not a hard quarantine), since a position
  1579	    # we cannot see at all only mis-states equity if it MOVES -> which surfaces as
  1580	    # inter-anchor drift and quarantines via the drift/missing-mark gate anyway.
  1581	    extradex_no_anchor = sorted(
  1582	        d for d in source_dexes
  1583	        if dex_in_scope(d) and d not in ANCHOR_COVERED_DEXES
  1584	    )
  1585	    has_extradex_no_anchor = bool(extradex_no_anchor)
  1586	
  1587	    # True series-start cumulative external flow (P1 fix: wr.ext_flow_cum is only
  1588	    # the per-SEGMENT flow since the active anchor and resets each anchor). We
  1589	    # accumulate ledger ext_flow over (effective_start, eod] independently.
  1590	    ledger_ext_events = sorted(
  1591	        (int(e["time"]), ledger_cash_delta(e, wallet_lc).ext_flow) for e in ledger
  1592	    )
  1593	
  1594	    # 3) Build the EOD series.
  1595	    earliest_anchor_ms = window_anchors[0][0] if window_anchors else valid_anchors[0][0]
  1596	    effective_start_ms = max(earliest_anchor_ms, start_ms)
  1597	    current_day = pd.Timestamp(effective_start_ms, unit="ms", tz="UTC").floor("D").date()
  1598	    end_day = pd.Timestamp(walk_end_ms, unit="ms", tz="UTC").floor("D").date()
  1599	
  1600	    rows: list[dict] = []
  1601	    had_unknown_any = False
  1602	    n_incomplete_rows = 0
  1603	    while current_day <= end_day:
  1604	        eod_ms = int(pd.Timestamp(current_day, tz="UTC").timestamp() * 1000 + 86_399_999)
  1605	        eod_ms = min(eod_ms, walk_end_ms)
  1606	        before = [(t, v) for t, v in window_anchors if t <= eod_ms]
  1607	        if not before:
  1608	            current_day = current_day + pd.Timedelta(days=1)
  1609	            continue
  1610	        anchor_t, anchor_v = before[-1]
  1611	        # SELECTION series: causal seed by default for downstream M5 eligibility.
