  1612	        # Anchor cash-snap marks remain point-in-time MTM via get_mark(..., causal=False).
  1613	        wr = compute_eq_at(
  1614	            stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, eod_ms, anchor_t, anchor_v,
  1615	            causal_seed=causal_seed,
  1616	        )
  1617	        had_unknown_any = had_unknown_any or wr.had_unknown_ledger
  1618	        # Row is incomplete only if we genuinely cannot value the book this day
  1619	        # (missing anchor/terminal mark). Touching a no-anchor-row dex is NOT an
  1620	        # incompleteness by itself (all dexes in scope) -> reconstruct it; hidden
  1621	        # exposure is caught by the inter-anchor drift quarantine below.
  1622	        row_incomplete = wr.recon_incomplete
  1623	        if row_incomplete:
  1624	            n_incomplete_rows += 1
  1625	
  1626	        # True cumulative external flow from series start through this EOD.
  1627	        ext_flow_cum_total = sum(
  1628	            fl for ts, fl in ledger_ext_events
  1629	            if effective_start_ms < ts <= eod_ms
  1630	        )
  1631	
  1632	        # return since the at-or-before weekly anchor for this day (NOT recon
  1633	        # drift: drift is only meaningful at the validation anchor checks).
  1634	        ret_pct = (wr.equity - anchor_v) / anchor_v if abs(anchor_v) > 0.01 else np.nan
  1635	        rows.append(
  1636	            {
  1637	                "wallet": wallet,
  1638	                "date": current_day,
  1639	                "equity_usd": wr.equity,
  1640	                "cash": wr.cash,
  1641	                "position_value_usd": wr.position_value,
  1642	                "gross_position_notional_usd": wr.gross_position_notional,
  1643	                "n_positions": len(wr.positions),
  1644	                "ext_flow_cum": ext_flow_cum_total,
  1645	                "segment_ext_flow": wr.ext_flow_cum,
  1646	                "n_unmarkable_positions": wr.n_unmarkable,
  1647	                "unmarkable_notional_usd": wr.unmarkable_notional,
  1648	                "anchor_age_h": (eod_ms - anchor_t) / 3_600_000,
  1649	                "return_since_anchor_pct": ret_pct,
  1650	                "recon_incomplete": row_incomplete,
  1651	                "has_liquidation_in_day": current_day in liq_days,
  1652	                "source_dexes": ",".join(source_dexes),
  1653	            }
  1654	        )
  1655	        current_day = current_day + pd.Timedelta(days=1)
  1656	
  1657	    if not rows:
  1658	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_rows"}
  1659	
  1660	    df_out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
  1661	
  1662	    # 4) Accuracy diagnostics MUST validate the exact seed mode used by the
  1663	    # emitted series.  Quarantine consumes inter-anchor drift, so using a later
  1664	    # snapshot-backed ex-post seed here while emitting causal rows would make
  1665	    # the acceptance test look better/different than its actual input artifact.
  1666	    inter = inter_anchor_drift(
  1667	        stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, window_anchors, causal_seed=causal_seed
  1668	    )
  1669	    recon = segment_reconcile(
  1670	        stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, window_anchors, fills,
  1671	        causal_seed=causal_seed,
  1672	    )
  1673	
  1674	    audit = {
  1675	        "n_fills": len(fills),
  1676	        "n_funding": len(funding),
  1677	        "n_ledger": len(ledger),
  1678	        "n_weekly_anchors_in_window": len(window_anchors),
  1679	        "n_sentinel_zeros": n_sentinel_zeros,
  1680	        "max_inter_anchor_drift_pct": inter["max_drift_pct"],
  1681	        "median_inter_anchor_drift_pct": inter["median_drift_pct"],
  1682	        "n_inter_anchor_checks": inter["n_checks"],
  1683	        "n_segments_reconciled": recon["n_segments"],
  1684	        "max_segment_reconcile_err_usd": recon["max_err_usd"],
  1685	        "median_segment_reconcile_err_usd": recon["median_err_usd"],
  1686	        "unknown_ledger_types": sorted(
  1687	            {
  1688	                e.get("delta", {}).get("type", "")
  1689	                for e in ledger
  1690	                if ledger_cash_delta(e, wallet_lc).unknown
  1691	            }
  1692	        ),
  1693	        "has_flx_anchor": anchor.has_flx_anchor,
  1694	        "has_unmarkable_dex_anchor": anchor.has_unmarkable_dex_anchor,
  1695	        "has_extradex_no_anchor": has_extradex_no_anchor,
  1696	        "extradex_no_anchor": ",".join(extradex_no_anchor),
  1697	        "has_unmarkable_in_series": bool(df_out["n_unmarkable_positions"].sum() > 0),
  1698	        "max_unmarkable_notional_usd": float(df_out["unmarkable_notional_usd"].max()),
  1699	        "n_incomplete_rows": n_incomplete_rows,
  1700	        "frac_incomplete_rows": float(n_incomplete_rows / len(df_out)) if len(df_out) else np.nan,
  1701	        "source_dexes": ",".join(source_dexes),
  1702	    }
  1703	    # Quarantine only when equity genuinely cannot be trusted: unknown ledger
  1704	    # types, OR a material fraction of rows had missing marks / incomplete seeding
  1705	    # (P0). All dexes are in scope now, so merely touching a no-anchor-row dex
  1706	    # (cash/hyna/para/vntl) is NOT a quarantine -> those positions reconstruct
  1707	    # from fills, and any unseen/moving exposure surfaces as inter-anchor drift.
  1708	    # Inter-anchor drift gate (codex backstop for hidden no-anchor-dex exposure):
  1709	    # an out-of-sample walk that lands >X% from the next weekly anchor means the
  1710	    # reconstruction is provably wrong for that wallet (e.g. a hidden position
  1711	    # absorbed into cash whose value moved). Quarantine on the proven error, not
  1712	    # on dex membership. NaN drift (all walks incomplete) is handled by frac below.
  1713	    _med = audit["median_inter_anchor_drift_pct"]
  1714	    _max = audit["max_inter_anchor_drift_pct"]
  1715	    drift_fail = (
  1716	        (_med == _med and _med > 0.10) or (_max == _max and _max > 0.50)
  1717	    )
  1718	    audit["quarantined"] = bool(
  1719	        had_unknown_any
  1720	        or audit["unknown_ledger_types"]
  1721	        or drift_fail
  1722	        or audit["n_inter_anchor_checks"] < MIN_INTER_ANCHOR_CHECKS
  1723	        or (audit["frac_incomplete_rows"] == audit["frac_incomplete_rows"]
  1724	            and audit["frac_incomplete_rows"] > 0.10)
  1725	    )
  1726	    audit["equity_validation_sufficient"] = bool(
  1727	        audit["n_inter_anchor_checks"] >= MIN_INTER_ANCHOR_CHECKS
  1728	    )
  1729	
  1730	    if validation_only:
  1731	        # Today cross-check + position validation compare reconstruction AT the
  1732	        # anchor-parquet fetch time against the parquet's own snapshot (same
  1733	        # timestamp = apples-to-apples). Position state needs fills up to
  1734	        # fetched_ms (which may be AFTER walk_end / mark coverage); positions
  1735	        # are mark-INDEPENDENT, so we load an extended fill set just for this.
  1736	        ext_fills = load_wallet_fills(wallet, start_ms, anchor.fetched_ms)
  1737	        ext_funding = load_wallet_funding(wallet, start_ms, anchor.fetched_ms)
  1738	        ext_ledger = load_wallet_ledger(wallet, start_ms, anchor.fetched_ms)
  1739	        ext_stream: list[tuple[int, str, dict]] = (
  1740	            [(f["time"], "fill", f) for f in ext_fills]
  1741	            + [(int(e["time"]), "ledger", e) for e in ext_ledger]
  1742	            + [(int(e["time"]), "funding", e) for e in ext_funding]
  1743	        )
  1744	        ext_stream.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
  1745	        cc = today_crosscheck(
  1746	            wallet, ext_stream, ext_fills, anchor, wallet_lc, window_anchors, df_out
  1747	        )
  1748	        pv = position_validation(
  1749	            ext_stream, ext_fills, anchor, wallet_lc, window_anchors
  1750	        )
  1751	        audit.update(cc)
  1752	        audit.update(pv)
  1753	
  1754	    return {"wallet": wallet, "series": df_out, "audit": audit}
  1755	
  1756	
  1757	def inter_anchor_drift(
  1758	    stream, fills, anchor: AnchorState, wallet_lc, window_anchors,
  1759	    causal_seed: bool = True,
  1760	) -> dict:
  1761	    """Accuracy #1: for each weekly anchor (except the first), reconstruct equity
  1762	    AT the current anchor's timestamp by walking forward from the PREVIOUS
  1763	    anchor, then compare to the current anchor's reported value. This is a
  1764	    genuine out-of-sample test of the walk: the measured point is the anchor
  1765	    itself, but the walk is seeded from the PRIOR anchor (no snap at cur_t).
  1766	
  1767	    Walks flagged incomplete (missing seed/mark) are excluded from the drift
  1768	    distribution -- those wallets are quarantined separately, not silently
  1769	    counted as low-drift."""
  1770	    drifts: list[float] = []
  1771	    for i in range(1, len(window_anchors)):
  1772	        prev_t, prev_v = window_anchors[i - 1]
  1773	        cur_t, cur_v = window_anchors[i]
  1774	        if cur_t <= prev_t:
  1775	            continue  # anchors at/within the same instant; nothing to test
  1776	        wr = compute_eq_at(
  1777	            stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, cur_t, prev_t, prev_v,
  1778	            causal_seed=causal_seed,
  1779	        )
  1780	        # compute_eq_at includes events at ts == cur_t (ts > anchor_ms AND
  1781	        # ts <= t_ms), matching HL's accountValue snapshot which is post-event.
  1782	        if wr.recon_incomplete:
  1783	            continue
  1784	        if abs(cur_v) > 0.01:
  1785	            drifts.append((wr.equity - cur_v) / cur_v)
  1786	    if not drifts:
  1787	        return {"max_drift_pct": np.nan, "median_drift_pct": np.nan, "n_checks": 0}
  1788	    a = np.abs(drifts)
  1789	    return {
  1790	        "max_drift_pct": float(a.max()),
  1791	        "median_drift_pct": float(np.median(a)),
  1792	        "n_checks": len(drifts),
  1793	    }
  1794	
  1795	
  1796	def segment_reconcile(
  1797	    stream, fills, anchor: AnchorState, wallet_lc, window_anchors, all_fills,
  1798	    causal_seed: bool = True,
  1799	) -> dict:
  1800	    """Accuracy #2: realized-PnL reconciliation per inter-anchor segment.
  1801	
  1802	    Self-financing identity (cross-check ONLY):
  1803	        (eq_{a+1} - eq_a) - ext_flow_segment
  1804	            ~= -sum(fees) + sum(funding)
  1805	                + delta(marked_position_value) - sum(signed_size * fill_price)
  1806	
  1807	    ``delta(position value) - net traded`` is TOTAL trading PnL, including
  1808	    realized PnL. Adding ``closedPnl`` again double-counts every close (a
  1809	    buy-100/sell-110 round trip became $20 instead of $10 in the old audit).
  1810	    ``closedPnl`` remains source data but is not part of this independent cash+
  1811	    marked-book identity.
  1812	    """
  1813	    errs: list[float] = []
  1814	    for i in range(1, len(window_anchors)):
  1815	        a_t, a_v = window_anchors[i - 1]
  1816	        b_t, b_v = window_anchors[i]
  1817	
  1818	        # ext_flow over the segment from the walk.
  1819	        ext = 0.0
  1820	        seg_fees = 0.0
  1821	        seg_funding = 0.0
  1822	        for ts, typ, ev in stream:
  1823	            if ts <= a_t or ts > b_t:
  1824	                continue
  1825	            if typ == "fill":
  1826	                # ``fee`` is the API total; builder/deployer fields are its
  1827	                # component breakdown and are not additional wallet charges.
  1828	                seg_fees += ev["fee"]
  1829	            elif typ == "funding":
  1830	                seg_funding += funding_cash_delta(ev)
  1831	            elif typ == "ledger":
  1832	                ext += ledger_cash_delta(ev, wallet_lc).ext_flow
  1833	
  1834	        # Total trading PnL: marked position value change minus the value put on
  1835	        # / taken off by fills. This already includes both realized and
  1836	        # unrealized PnL and is independent of the source closedPnl field.
  1837	        wr_a = compute_eq_at(
  1838	            stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, a_t, a_t, a_v,
  1839	            causal_seed=causal_seed,
  1840	        )
  1841	        wr_b = compute_eq_at(
  1842	            stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, b_t, a_t, a_v,
  1843	            causal_seed=causal_seed,
  1844	        )
  1845	        # net cash paid into positions over the segment (sign: buying costs cash)
  1846	        net_traded = 0.0
  1847	        for ts, typ, ev in stream:
  1848	            if ts <= a_t or ts > b_t:
  1849	                continue
  1850	            if typ == "fill":
  1851	                net_traded += ev["signed_sz"] * ev["price"]
  1852	        trading_pnl = (wr_b.position_value - wr_a.position_value) - net_traded
  1853	
  1854	        lhs = (b_v - a_v) - ext
  1855	        rhs = -seg_fees + seg_funding + trading_pnl
  1856	        errs.append(abs(lhs - rhs))
  1857	
  1858	    if not errs:
  1859	        return {"n_segments": 0, "max_err_usd": np.nan, "median_err_usd": np.nan}
  1860	    return {
  1861	        "n_segments": len(errs),
  1862	        "max_err_usd": float(np.max(errs)),
  1863	        "median_err_usd": float(np.median(errs)),
  1864	    }
  1865	
  1866	
  1867	def today_crosscheck(
  1868	    wallet, ext_stream, ext_fills, anchor: AnchorState, wallet_lc, window_anchors, df_out
  1869	) -> dict:
  1870	    """Accuracy #3: cross-check the reconstruction against authoritative snapshots.
  1871	
  1872	    Two references are reported:
  1873	      - main_xyz_anchor_snapshot_pct: WHOLE-ACCOUNT reconstruction AT
  1874	        anchor.fetched_ms vs the anchor parquet's aggregate accountValue. NOTE
  1875	        the parquet aggregate is MAIN+XYZ ONLY (it has no cash/hyna/para/vntl
  1876	        rows), so for any extra-dex wallet this intentionally compares a
  1877	        whole-account number to a main+xyz partial -> it is only meaningful for
  1878	        pure main/xyz wallets and is LABELLED as such. The whole-account clean
  1879	        check is today_crosscheck_pct below.
  1880	      - today_crosscheck_pct: same reconstruction vs the LIVE clearinghouseState
  1881	        sum-of-ALL-dexes accountValue (fetched now). This is the genuine
  1882	        whole-account cross-check; it carries whatever the account moved between
  1883	        the parquet snapshot and now, so it is time-stale but dex-complete.
  1884	
  1885	    Note: marks past 2026-05-23 do not exist, so position value at fetched_ms is
  1886	    marked at the last available candle. For the anchor_snapshot cross-check this
  1887	    introduces a mark-staleness term equal to position drift over (markEnd ->
  1888	    fetch), which we surface but cannot remove without oracle marks (future work).
  1889	    """
  1890	    out: dict = {}
  1891	    # Reconstruction at the parquet fetch time from the last in-window anchor.
  1892	    recon_at_fetch = np.nan
  1893	    if window_anchors:
  1894	        a_t, a_v = window_anchors[-1]
  1895	        wr = compute_eq_at(
  1896	            ext_stream, ext_fills, anchor, wallet_lc, anchor.fetched_ms, a_t, a_v
  1897	        )
  1898	        recon_at_fetch = wr.equity
  1899	    out["recon_equity_at_fetch_usd"] = recon_at_fetch
  1900	    out["anchor_aggregate_acct_value_usd"] = anchor.aggregate_acct_value
  1901	    if anchor.aggregate_acct_value and abs(anchor.aggregate_acct_value) > 0.01 and recon_at_fetch == recon_at_fetch:
  1902	        out["main_xyz_anchor_snapshot_pct"] = (
  1903	            recon_at_fetch - anchor.aggregate_acct_value
  1904	        ) / anchor.aggregate_acct_value
  1905	    else:
  1906	        out["main_xyz_anchor_snapshot_pct"] = np.nan
  1907	
  1908	    # Live clearinghouse sum over ALL perp dexes (informational; time-stale by
  1909	    # design). Built from the known HIP-3 set UNION the dexes this wallet was
  1910	    # actually seen on (anchor parquet), so we never miss a dex it trades. main
  1911	    # is queried as dex=None.
  1912	    KNOWN_PERP_DEXES = {"xyz", "cash", "hyna", "para", "vntl", "flx", "km"}
  1913	    seen = {d for d in anchor.dexes_seen if d and d != "main"}
  1914	    dexes = [None] + sorted(KNOWN_PERP_DEXES | seen)
  1915	    total = 0.0
  1916	    got_any = False
  1917	    for dex in dexes:
  1918	        cs = get_clearinghouse(wallet, dex)
  1919	        if not cs:
  1920	            continue
  1921	        av = cs.get("marginSummary", {}).get("accountValue")
  1922	        if av is not None:
  1923	            total += float(av)
  1924	            got_any = True
  1925	    out["today_live_equity_usd"] = total if got_any else np.nan
  1926	    if got_any and recon_at_fetch == recon_at_fetch and abs(total) > 0.01:
  1927	        out["today_crosscheck_pct"] = (recon_at_fetch - total) / total
  1928	    else:
  1929	        out["today_crosscheck_pct"] = np.nan
  1930	    return out
  1931	
  1932	
  1933	def position_validation(
  1934	    stream, fills, anchor: AnchorState, wallet_lc, window_anchors
  1935	) -> dict:
  1936	    """Accuracy #4: reconstructed per-coin positions AT the anchor-parquet fetch
  1937	    time vs the parquet positions_json (main+xyz). Both reference the SAME
  1938	    timestamp (anchor.fetched_ms), so the extended fill stream (loaded to
  1939	    fetched_ms) is required — positions are mark-independent.
  1940	
  1941	    Reports per-coin mismatch count (rel error > 1% AND abs error > 1e-6) plus
  1942	    the worst absolute size error for diagnostics.
  1943	    """
  1944	    if not window_anchors:
  1945	        return {
  1946	            "n_position_mismatches": np.nan,
  1947	            "n_positions_checked": 0,
  1948	            "max_position_abs_err": np.nan,
  1949	            "position_validation_status": "no_anchor",
  1950	        }
  1951	    # The anchor parquet snapshots positions at anchor.fetched_ms. Our local S3
  1952	    # fills may end BEFORE fetched_ms (the daily parquets lag the anchor pull).
  1953	    # If so, reconstructed positions reflect an earlier moment than the parquet
  1954	    # snapshot and the comparison is cross-time / not meaningful. Detect and flag.
  1955	    last_fill_ms = max((f["time"] for f in fills), default=0)
  1956	    gap_h = (anchor.fetched_ms - last_fill_ms) / 3_600_000 if last_fill_ms else float("inf")
  1957	    if gap_h > 24:
  1958	        return {
  1959	            "n_position_mismatches": np.nan,
  1960	            "n_positions_checked": 0,
  1961	            "max_position_abs_err": np.nan,
  1962	            "position_validation_status": f"fills_end_{gap_h:.0f}h_before_anchor",
  1963	        }
  1964	    a_t, a_v = window_anchors[-1]
  1965	    wr = compute_eq_at(stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, anchor.fetched_ms, a_t, a_v)
  1966	    recon = wr.positions
  1967	    mismatches = 0
  1968	    checked = 0
  1969	    max_abs_err = 0.0
  1970	    coins = set(recon) | set(anchor.positions)
  1971	    for c in coins:
  1972	        if coin_is_dropped(c) or coin_is_spot(c):
  1973	            continue
  1974	        r = recon.get(c, 0.0)
  1975	        a = float(anchor.positions.get(c, 0.0))
  1976	        checked += 1
  1977	        err = abs(r - a)
  1978	        max_abs_err = max(max_abs_err, err)
  1979	        denom = max(abs(a), abs(r), 1e-6)
  1980	        if err > 1e-6 and err / denom > 0.01:
  1981	            mismatches += 1
  1982	    return {
  1983	        "n_position_mismatches": mismatches,
  1984	        "n_positions_checked": checked,
  1985	        "max_position_abs_err": max_abs_err,
  1986	        "position_validation_status": "ok",
  1987	    }
  1988	
  1989	
  1990	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1991	# M02 BRIDGE — additive per-event equity series (NO reconstruction-MATH change)
  1992	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1993	#
  1994	# This block is ADDITIVE for V15 M02 (journey_trace). It does NOT touch the
  1995	# existing daily/audit reconstruction above; it reuses the SAME helpers
  1996	# (seed_positions, get_mark, fill/ledger/funding cash deltas) to emit, per
  1997	# wallet, a PER-EVENT equity_post series in the TOTAL EVENT ORDER required by
  1998	# the M02 design spec:
  1999	#
  2000	#   event_order key = (ts_ms, type_rank, source_seq)
  2001	#     type_rank: ledger=0, fill=1, funding=2   (ledger settles, then trade,
  2002	#                then funding accrues on the post-trade position)
  2003	#     source_seq: stable causal fill sequence; per-type append index otherwise.
  2004	#
  2005	# For each event k we re-anchor to the last weekly anchor with anchor_ts
  2006	# STRICTLY < ts(k) (codex r3: an anchor sharing the action's ms is NOT a valid
  2007	# base), walk forward applying every event with order <= k (incl. this one),
  2008	# then mark-to-market at ts(k). equity_post(k) therefore uses ONLY data with
  2009	# order <= k — no look-ahead. We also emit the per-event reliability fields M02
  2010	# needs to pick its equity-basis mode (markable_all, n_unmarkable, frozen
  2011	# component value/age, age_since_anchor, mark_ts, anchor_ts) — all derived from
  2012	# data <= k only.
  2013	
  2014	# type_rank for the total event order (codex r3/r4).
  2015	_TYPE_RANK = {"ledger": 0, "fill": 1, "funding": 2}
  2016	
  2017	# STALE_CAP: fall back if reconstruction base anchor is older than one anchor
  2018	# cycle (7d) while material unmarkable exposure exists. Tunable (spec).
  2019	STALE_CAP_MS = 7 * 86_400_000
  2020	
  2021	SPOT_USDC_INVARIANT_NOTE = (
  2022	    "HL is a unified wallet: spot USDC IS perp margin. The weekly perpAllTime "
  2023	    "anchor (marginSummary.accountValue summed across perp dexes) already "
  2024	    "includes collateral (USDC) + uPnL. Re-anchoring snaps cash := anchor_eq - "
  2025	    "Sum pos*mark, so the spot-USDC/collateral component is captured inside "
  2026	    "`cash` and carried causally by ledger deposit/withdraw/transfer deltas. "
  2027	    "equity_post is therefore whole-account (collateral-inclusive) by "
  2028	    "construction; see test_m01_spot_collateral_invariant."
  2029	)
  2030	
  2031	
  2032	def build_event_stream(
  2033	    fills: list[dict],
  2034	    funding: list[dict],
  2035	    ledger: list[dict],
  2036	) -> list[dict]:
  2037	    """Merge fills+funding+ledger into the TOTAL EVENT ORDER (spec v4).
  2038	
  2039	    Returns a list of event dicts each carrying:
  2040	        ts, type ('fill'|'funding'|'ledger'), type_rank, source_seq, ev (payload)
  2041	    sorted by (ts, type_rank, source_seq). source_seq is the causal per-wallet
  2042	    fill sequence, else a stable per-type append index so the order is deterministic
  2043	    and reproducible across M01 (equity) and M02 (actions).
  2044	
  2045	    SAME-MS FILL ORDER: tid is identity, not sequence. Partial fills are ordered
  2046	    by chaining startPosition -> startPosition+signed_size. The fill payload is
  2047	    carried directly so M2 never rematches on a lossy key.
  2048	    """
  2049	    out: list[dict] = []
  2050	    ordered_fills = order_wallet_fills_causally(list(fills))
  2051	    for i, f in enumerate(ordered_fills):
  2052	        if not coin_is_allowed_perp(f["coin"]):
  2053	            continue
  2054	        seq = int(f.get("fill_seq", i))
  2055	        out.append(
  2056	            {
  2057	                "ts": int(f["time"]),
  2058	                "type": "fill",
  2059	                "type_rank": _TYPE_RANK["fill"],
  2060	                "source_seq": seq,
  2061	                "ev": f,
  2062	            }
  2063	        )
  2064	    for i, e in enumerate(ledger):
  2065	        out.append(
  2066	            {
  2067	                "ts": int(e["time"]),
  2068	                "type": "ledger",
  2069	                "type_rank": _TYPE_RANK["ledger"],
  2070	                "source_seq": i,
  2071	                "ev": e,
  2072	            }
  2073	        )
  2074	    for i, e in enumerate(funding):
  2075	        out.append(
  2076	            {
  2077	                "ts": int(e["time"]),
  2078	                "type": "funding",
  2079	                "type_rank": _TYPE_RANK["funding"],
  2080	                "source_seq": i,
  2081	                "ev": e,
  2082	            }
  2083	        )
  2084	    out.sort(key=lambda x: (x["ts"], x["type_rank"], x["source_seq"]))
  2085	    return out
  2086	
  2087	
  2088	@dataclass
  2089	class EventEquity:
  2090	    """Per-event reconstruction output (whole-account, causal)."""
  2091	
  2092	    event_order: int           # strictly monotone index in the ordered stream
  2093	    ts: int
  2094	    type: str
  2095	    source_seq: int
  2096	    equity_post: float         # whole-account equity AFTER this event
  2097	    cash: float
  2098	    position_value: float
  2099	    anchor_ts: Optional[int]   # base anchor (strictly < ts); None if no past anchor
  2100	    anchor_equity: Optional[float]
  2101	    age_since_anchor_ms: Optional[int]
  2102	    mark_ts: Optional[int]     # latest mark <= ts across marked positions
  2103	    markable_all: bool         # every held nonzero position markable at ts
  2104	    n_unmarkable: int
  2105	    no_extradex_without_anchor: bool
  2106	    frozen_component_value: float   # last-known value of unmarkable positions
  2107	    frozen_component_age_ms: int    # staleness of the frozen value at ts
  2108	    has_past_anchor: bool
  2109	    fill: Optional[dict] = None     # for type=='fill': the EXACT fill payload of
  2110	                                    # this event (codex r4 BUG 2) — M02 consumes it
  2111	                                    # directly off the stream, never rematching by
  2112	                                    # (ts, tid). None for funding/ledger events.
  2113	
  2114	
  2115	def compute_event_equity(
  2116	    ordered: list[dict],
  2117	    fills: list[dict],
  2118	    anchor: "AnchorState",
  2119	    wallet_lc: str,
  2120	    window_anchors: list[tuple[int, float]],
  2121	    extradex_no_anchor: bool,
  2122	    causal_mark: bool = True,
  2123	) -> list[EventEquity]:
  2124	    """Walk the ordered event stream ONCE and emit equity_post per event.
  2125	
  2126	    Causal contract (spec NON-LOOK-AHEAD INVARIANTS):
  2127	      * base anchor for event k = last weekly anchor with anchor_ts STRICTLY < ts(k).
  2128	      * equity_post(k) = cash + Sum pos*mark(ts(k)) using ONLY events order <= k.
  2129	        By default the terminal live-position MTM uses closed causal marks so
  2130	        M02's sizing denominator matches its causal numerator.
  2131	      * mark_ts <= ts(k); anchor_ts < ts(k); event_order strictly monotone.
  2132	
  2133	    We re-seed/re-snap cash whenever the active base anchor changes (each weekly
  2134	    anchor cycle), exactly like compute_eq_at, then incrementally apply each
  2135	    event's cash/position delta. Mark-to-market is recomputed at each event ts
  2136	    over the live positions, tracking unmarkable positions' last-known value
  2137	    (the FROZEN component) so M02 can do PARTIAL_MTM without look-ahead.
  2138	    """
  2139	    results: list[EventEquity] = []
  2140	
  2141	    # Active base-anchor state. Re-initialised lazily when the base anchor for
  2142	    # the current event differs from the one in force.
  2143	    active_anchor_ts: Optional[int] = None
  2144	    active_anchor_eq: Optional[float] = None
  2145	    cash = 0.0
  2146	    positions: dict[str, float] = {}
  2147	    # last-known mark value per coin (for the frozen unmarkable component)
  2148	    last_val: dict[str, float] = {}
  2149	    last_val_ts: dict[str, int] = {}
  2150	    # Coins whose carry-in (pre-anchor holding) has already been folded into the
  2151	    # book+cash under the ACTIVE anchor. Reset per anchor cycle (codex r4 BUG 1).
  2152	    carried_in: set[str] = set()
  2153	
  2154	    def _carry_in_fill(ev: dict) -> None:
  2155	        """CAUSAL replacement for seed_positions case 2 (codex r4 BUG 1).
  2156	
  2157	        seed_positions(causal_cutoff=True) deliberately omits a coin first traded
  2158	        AFTER the active anchor, to avoid look-ahead at earlier events. But that
  2159	        coin DID hold a pre-anchor position (its first fill's startPosition), which
  2160	        the anchor equity a_v already includes. The first time we REACH that coin's
  2161	        fill in the ordered walk (order <= k = causal), we fold its carry-in in the
  2162	        SAME way case 2 + the cash snap would have: add startPosition to the book
  2163	        AND subtract its anchor-time value from cash (mark at active_anchor_ts < ts,
  2164	        causal) so equity is not double-counted. Coins already in the anchor seed,
  2165	        or already folded, are skipped. Identical end-state to compute_eq_at, but
  2166	        revealed only at the causal moment instead of at the anchor (no leak at
  2167	        earlier events)."""
  2168	        nonlocal cash
  2169	        coin = ev["coin"]
  2170	        if coin in carried_in or active_anchor_ts is None:
  2171	            return
  2172	        carried_in.add(coin)
  2173	        sp = float(ev.get("startPosition", 0.0) or 0.0)
  2174	        if abs(sp) <= 1e-9:
  2175	            return
  2176	        positions[coin] = positions.get(coin, 0.0) + sp
  2177	        m = get_mark(coin, active_anchor_ts)
  2178	        if m is not None:
  2179	            cash -= sp * m  # mirror the anchor cash snap for this carried-in coin
  2180	            last_val[coin] = sp * m
  2181	            last_val_ts.setdefault(coin, active_anchor_ts)
  2182	
  2183	    def _past_anchor(ts: int) -> Optional[tuple[int, float]]:
  2184	        chosen = None
  2185	        for a_t, a_v in window_anchors:
  2186	            if a_t < ts:            # STRICT (codex r3)
  2187	                chosen = (a_t, a_v)
  2188	            else:
  2189	                break
  2190	        return chosen
  2191	
  2192	    for order, item in enumerate(ordered):
  2193	        ts = item["ts"]
  2194	        base = _past_anchor(ts)
  2195	
  2196	        if base is None:
  2197	            # No past anchor → cannot reconstruct a causal denominator. Still
  2198	            # advance the position book from startPosition-derived seeding so
  2199	            # that once an anchor appears the book is correct, but emit
  2200	            # has_past_anchor=False (M02 maps this to NO_ANCHOR).
  2201	            # We seed positions at this ts from fills only (causal: positions_at
  2202	            # uses startPosition + cumulative signed_sz, all <= ts).
  2203	            ev = item["ev"]
  2204	            if item["type"] == "fill":
  2205	                positions[ev["coin"]] = positions.get(ev["coin"], 0.0) + ev["signed_sz"]
  2206	                if abs(positions[ev["coin"]]) < 1e-9:
  2207	                    positions.pop(ev["coin"], None)
  2208	            results.append(
  2209	                EventEquity(
  2210	                    event_order=order, ts=ts, type=item["type"],
  2211	                    source_seq=item["source_seq"], equity_post=float("nan"),
  2212	                    cash=float("nan"), position_value=float("nan"),
  2213	                    anchor_ts=None, anchor_equity=None, age_since_anchor_ms=None,
  2214	                    mark_ts=None, markable_all=False, n_unmarkable=0,
  2215	                    no_extradex_without_anchor=(not extradex_no_anchor),
  2216	                    frozen_component_value=0.0, frozen_component_age_ms=0,
  2217	                    has_past_anchor=False,
  2218	                    fill=(item["ev"] if item["type"] == "fill" else None),
  2219	                )
  2220	            )
  2221	            continue
  2222	
  2223	        a_t, a_v = base
  2224	        if a_t != active_anchor_ts:
  2225	            # New base anchor cycle → re-seed positions at the anchor and snap
  2226	            # cash, identically to compute_eq_at. This rebuild uses only fills
  2227	            # (startPosition + cumulative) at/<= anchor_ts, which is < ts → causal.
  2228	            active_anchor_ts = a_t
  2229	            active_anchor_eq = a_v
  2230	            seeded = seed_positions(fills, anchor, a_t, causal_cutoff=True)
  2231	            anchor_pos_value = 0.0
  2232	            for c, sz in seeded.items():
  2233	                if abs(sz) < 1e-9:
  2234	                    continue
  2235	                m = get_mark(c, a_t)
  2236	                if m is not None:
  2237	                    anchor_pos_value += sz * m
  2238	                    last_val[c] = sz * m
  2239	                    last_val_ts[c] = a_t
  2240	            cash = a_v - anchor_pos_value
  2241	            positions = {c: s for c, s in seeded.items() if abs(s) > 1e-9}
  2242	            # Coins already in the anchor seed are carried-in; their fills must NOT
  2243	            # re-fold a startPosition. Coins NOT seeded (first traded after anchor)
  2244	            # get folded causally at their first fill via _carry_in_fill.
  2245	            carried_in = set(seeded.keys())
  2246	            # Replay events strictly between the anchor and this event (anchor_ts
  2247	            # < e_ts < ts) so the book/cash reflect everything order <= current.
  2248	            for prev in ordered:
  2249	                p_ts = prev["ts"]
  2250	                if p_ts <= a_t:
  2251	                    continue
  2252	                # stop once we reach the current event's order position
  2253	                if (p_ts, prev["type_rank"], prev["source_seq"]) >= (
  2254	                    ts, item["type_rank"], item["source_seq"]
  2255	                ):
  2256	                    break
  2257	                if prev["type"] == "fill":
  2258	                    _carry_in_fill(prev["ev"])
  2259	                _apply(prev, positions, last_val, last_val_ts, _cash_ref=None)
  2260	                cash = _apply_cash(prev, cash, wallet_lc)
  2261	
  2262	        # Apply THIS event (order == k included).
  2263	        if item["type"] == "fill":
  2264	            _carry_in_fill(item["ev"])
  2265	        _apply(item, positions, last_val, last_val_ts, _cash_ref=None)
  2266	        cash = _apply_cash(item, cash, wallet_lc)
  2267	
  2268	        # Mark-to-market at ts over live positions; track frozen component.
  2269	        pos_value = 0.0
  2270	        n_unmarkable = 0
  2271	        mark_ts_latest: Optional[int] = None
  2272	        frozen_value = 0.0
  2273	        frozen_age = 0
  2274	        for c, sz in positions.items():
  2275	            if abs(sz) < 1e-9:
  2276	                continue
  2277	            m = get_mark(c, ts, causal=causal_mark)
  2278	            if m is None:
  2279	                n_unmarkable += 1
  2280	                fv = last_val.get(c, 0.0)
  2281	                frozen_value += fv
  2282	                if c in last_val_ts:
  2283	                    frozen_age = max(frozen_age, ts - last_val_ts[c])
  2284	                continue
  2285	            val = sz * m
  2286	            pos_value += val
  2287	            last_val[c] = val
  2288	            mk = ts // 60_000 * 60_000 - (60_000 if causal_mark else 0)
  2289	            last_val_ts[c] = mk
  2290	            mark_ts_latest = mk if mark_ts_latest is None else max(mark_ts_latest, mk)
  2291	
  2292	        equity_post = cash + pos_value  # markable + cash; frozen excluded here
  2293	        results.append(
  2294	            EventEquity(
  2295	                event_order=order, ts=ts, type=item["type"],
  2296	                source_seq=item["source_seq"],
  2297	                equity_post=equity_post + frozen_value,  # whole-account incl frozen
  2298	                cash=cash, position_value=pos_value,
  2299	                anchor_ts=a_t, anchor_equity=a_v,
  2300	                age_since_anchor_ms=ts - a_t,
  2301	                mark_ts=mark_ts_latest if mark_ts_latest is not None else (ts if not positions else None),
  2302	                markable_all=(n_unmarkable == 0),
  2303	                n_unmarkable=n_unmarkable,
  2304	                no_extradex_without_anchor=(not extradex_no_anchor),
  2305	                frozen_component_value=frozen_value,
  2306	                frozen_component_age_ms=frozen_age,
  2307	                has_past_anchor=True,
  2308	                fill=(item["ev"] if item["type"] == "fill" else None),
  2309	            )
  2310	        )
  2311	    return results
  2312	
  2313	
  2314	def _apply(item: dict, positions: dict, last_val: dict, last_val_ts: dict, _cash_ref=None) -> None:
  2315	    """Apply an event's POSITION delta (cash handled by _apply_cash)."""
  2316	    if item["type"] == "fill":
  2317	        ev = item["ev"]
  2318	        coin = ev["coin"]
  2319	        positions[coin] = positions.get(coin, 0.0) + ev["signed_sz"]
  2320	        if abs(positions[coin]) < 1e-9:
  2321	            positions.pop(coin, None)
  2322	
  2323	
  2324	def _apply_cash(item: dict, cash: float, wallet_lc: str) -> float:
  2325	    """Apply an event's CASH delta (mirrors compute_eq_at's walk)."""
  2326	    t = item["type"]
  2327	    ev = item["ev"]
  2328	    if t == "fill":
  2329	        return cash + fill_cash_delta(ev)
  2330	    if t == "ledger":
  2331	        return cash + ledger_cash_delta(ev, wallet_lc).cash
  2332	    if t == "funding":
  2333	        return cash + funding_cash_delta(ev)
  2334	    return cash
  2335	
  2336	
  2337	def reconstruct_wallet_event_equity(args: tuple) -> dict:
  2338	    """M02 bridge driver: emit the per-event equity series + anchors for one wallet.
  2339	
  2340	    Returns {'wallet', 'events': [EventEquity...], 'weekly_anchors': [(ts,eq)],
  2341	    'fills': [...], 'funding': [...], 'inter_anchor_drift': {...} (diagnostic
  2342	    only, NEVER used to switch modes), 'extradex_no_anchor': bool}.
  2343	    Mirrors reconstruct_wallet's loading + ordering exactly so equity_post is
  2344	    consistent with the audited daily reconstruction.
  2345	    """
  2346	    wallet, anchor, start_ms, end_ms = args
  2347	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
  2348	    if anchor is None:
  2349	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_anchor"}
  2350	
  2351	    avh = get_portfolio_perp(wallet)
  2352	    valid_anchors = [(t, v) for t, v in avh if v > 0.01]
  2353	    if not valid_anchors:
  2354	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_valid_anchors"}
  2355	
  2356	    walk_end_ms = min(end_ms, anchor.fetched_ms)
  2357	    load_end_ms = max(walk_end_ms, int(anchor.fetched_ms))
  2358	
  2359	    fills = load_wallet_fills(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  2360	    funding = load_wallet_funding(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  2361	    ledger = load_wallet_ledger(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  2362	
  2363	    window_anchors = [(t, v) for t, v in valid_anchors if start_ms <= t <= walk_end_ms]
  2364	    if not window_anchors:
  2365	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_window_anchors"}
  2366	
  2367	    source_dexes = sorted({coin_dex(f["coin"]) for f in fills} | {
  2368	        coin_dex(c) for c in anchor.positions
  2369	    })
  2370	    extradex_no_anchor = bool([
  2371	        d for d in source_dexes
  2372	        if dex_in_scope(d) and d not in ANCHOR_COVERED_DEXES
  2373	    ])
  2374	
  2375	    ordered = build_event_stream(fills, funding, ledger)
  2376	    # Only emit events up to walk_end_ms (mark coverage); load_end may exceed it
  2377	    # but those tail events have no marks and are not sizing-relevant for M02.
  2378	    ordered = [e for e in ordered if e["ts"] <= walk_end_ms]
  2379	
  2380	    events = compute_event_equity(
  2381	        ordered, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, window_anchors, extradex_no_anchor
  2382	    )
  2383	
  2384	    # diagnostic only (never a mode switch): reuse the existing M01 drift calc.
  2385	    stream = [(e["ts"], e["type"], e["ev"]) for e in ordered]
  2386	    inter = inter_anchor_drift(stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, window_anchors)
  2387	
  2388	    return {
  2389	        "wallet": wallet,
  2390	        "events": events,
  2391	        "weekly_anchors": window_anchors,
  2392	        "fills": fills,
  2393	        "funding": funding,
  2394	        "extradex_no_anchor": extradex_no_anchor,
  2395	        "inter_anchor_drift": inter,
  2396	    }
  2397	
  2398	
  2399	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  2400	# Main
  2401	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  2402	
  2403	
  2404	def _print_accuracy_table(results: list[dict]) -> None:
  2405	    """Pretty-print the per-wallet accuracy table (validate mode)."""
  2406	    logger.info("\n" + "=" * 124)
  2407	    logger.info("PER-WALLET ACCURACY TABLE")
  2408	    logger.info(
  2409	        "  inter_drift = forward-walk recon vs NEXT weekly anchor (out-of-sample). "
  2410	        "snap% = recon@fetch vs anchor-parquet aggregate (same ts). "
  2411	        "today% = recon@fetch vs LIVE clearinghouse (time-stale, informational)."
  2412	    )
  2413	    logger.info("=" * 124)
  2414	    hdr = (
  2415	        f"{'wallet':<16} {'dexes':<22} {'#anch':>5} {'inter_drift med/max%':>22} "
  2416	        f"{'seg_err med/max $':>20} {'snap%':>8} {'today%':>8} {'pos_mis':>8} {'quar':>5}"
  2417	    )
  2418	    logger.info(hdr)
  2419	    logger.info("-" * 124)
  2420	    for r in results:
  2421	        w = r["wallet"]
  2422	        if "error" in r:
  2423	            logger.info(f"{w[:14]:<16} ERROR: {r['error']}")
  2424	            continue
  2425	        a = r["audit"]
  2426	        med = a.get("median_inter_anchor_drift_pct", np.nan)
  2427	        mx = a.get("max_inter_anchor_drift_pct", np.nan)
  2428	        smed = a.get("median_segment_reconcile_err_usd", np.nan)
  2429	        smx = a.get("max_segment_reconcile_err_usd", np.nan)
  2430	        snap = a.get("main_xyz_anchor_snapshot_pct", np.nan)
  2431	        today = a.get("today_crosscheck_pct", np.nan)
  2432	        posmis = a.get("n_position_mismatches", np.nan)
  2433	        dexes = a.get("source_dexes", "")
  2434	
  2435	        def _p(x):
  2436	            return f"{x*100:.2f}" if x == x else "n/a"  # NaN check
  2437	
  2438	        def _d(x):
  2439	            return f"{x:.2f}" if x == x else "n/a"
  2440	
  2441	        logger.info(
  2442	            f"{w[:14]:<16} {dexes[:20]:<22} "
  2443	            f"{a.get('n_weekly_anchors_in_window', 0):>5} "
  2444	            f"{_p(med)+'/'+_p(mx):>22} "
  2445	            f"{_d(smed)+'/'+_d(smx):>20} "
  2446	            f"{_p(snap):>8} "
  2447	            f"{_p(today):>8} "
  2448	            f"{str(posmis):>8} "
  2449	            f"{('Y' if a.get('quarantined') else '-'):>5}"
  2450	        )
  2451	    logger.info("=" * 124)
  2452	
  2453	
  2454	def main() -> None:
  2455	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="V15 M01 whole-account equity reconstructor")
  2456	    ap.add_argument("--wallets-file", required=True, help="One wallet address per line")
  2457	    ap.add_argument("--start", default="2025-12-01", help="YYYY-MM-DD")
  2458	    ap.add_argument("--end", default="2026-05-23", help="YYYY-MM-DD (clamped to mark coverage)")
  2459	    ap.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Output parquet path")
  2460	    ap.add_argument("--validate", action="store_true", help="Print accuracy table")
  2461	    ap.add_argument(
  2462	        "--ex-post-seed",
  2463	        action="store_true",
  2464	        help=(
  2465	            "Use legacy ex-post position seeding for the emitted EOD selection series "
  2466	            "(diagnostic studies only; not deployable). Default is causal seeding. "
  2467	            "Audit diagnostics always use the same mode as the emitted series."
  2468	        ),
  2469	    )
  2470	    ap.add_argument("--headroom-gb", type=float, default=6.0,
  2471	                    help="RAM reserved for the live baseline (agents + mongod + postgres); the main "
  2472	                         "memory-guard soft cap is lowered to (free - headroom) when RAM is tight.")
  2473	    args = ap.parse_args()
  2474	
  2475	    # Rule 8 (mandatory streaming I/O): abort LOUDLY on runaway RSS instead of silent OS SIGKILL.
  2476	    # 2026-06-10 OOM fix: m01 is single-process, so model the process itself as the one "worker"
  2477	    # (main_reserve=0). Aborts BEFORE work if the box cannot fit it; else lowers the main guard to
  2478	    # what is grantable (<= free - headroom) so it cannot exhaust a box running the live baseline.
  2479	    _budget = plan_memory_budget(requested_procs=1, per_worker_gb=2.0, main_reserve_gb=0.0,
  2480	                                 headroom_gb=args.headroom_gb, main_soft_cap=12.0)
  2481	    install_memory_guard(soft_gb=_budget.main_soft_gb, label="m01")
  2482	
  2483	    start_ms = int(pd.Timestamp(args.start, tz="UTC").timestamp() * 1000)
  2484	    end_ms = int((pd.Timestamp(args.end, tz="UTC") + pd.Timedelta(days=1)).timestamp() * 1000 - 1)
  2485

  2488	            line.strip().lower()

  2491	        ]

  2508	    uniq_dates: set = set()

  2514	            res = reconstruct_wallet((w, anchor, start_ms, end_ms, args.validate, not args.ex_post_seed))
  2515	        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001

  2518

  2521	            results_lite.append({"wallet": res["wallet"], "error": res["error"]})
  2522	        else:
  2523	            df_w = res["series"]

  2526	                f"inter-drift med {res['audit'].get('median_inter_anchor_drift_pct', float('nan'))*100:.2f}%"
  2527	            )
  2528	            # stream this wallet's rows to disk, then drop the DataFrame (bounded RAM)
  2529	            if len(df_w):
  2530	                series_writer.add_many(df_w.to_dict("records"))
  2531	                n_with_series += 1
  2532	                uniq_wallets.add(w)
  2533	                uniq_dates.update(df_w["date"].tolist())
  2534	            audits.append({"wallet": res["wallet"], **res["audit"]})
  2535	            results_lite.append({"wallet": res["wallet"], "audit": res["audit"]})
  2536	
  2537	    if args.validate:
  2538	        _print_accuracy_table(results_lite)
  2539	
  2540	    if n_with_series:
  2541	        total_rows = series_writer.close()
  2542	        audit_df = pd.DataFrame(audits)
  2543	        audit_df.to_parquet(out_path.with_suffix(".audit.parquet"), index=False, compression="snappy")
  2544	        logger.info(
  2545	            f"\nWrote {out_path}: {total_rows:,} rows "
  2546	            f"({len(uniq_wallets)} wallets x {len(uniq_dates)} days)"
  2547	        )
  2548	        logger.info(f"Wrote audit: {out_path.with_suffix('.audit.parquet')}")

  2551	        # EMPTY no-schema parquet to out_path and CLOBBER a prior valid artifact. abort() discards the
  2552	        # (empty) staging parts and leaves out_path untouched -> the old artifact is preserved.
  2553	        series_writer.abort()

  2555	        sys.exit(2)

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Build equity-independent, causally ordered perp actions from enriched fills.
     3	
     4	This lane is suitable for fixed-notional or direction-only copy research. It
     5	does not reconstruct leader equity and never emits exposure percentages.
     6	
     7	The default output is one row per source fill because the live trade stream is
     8	also fill-by-fill.  Same-millisecond burst rows are available as a diagnostic,
     9	but must not drive a fidelity replay: netting a burst can erase an intraburst
    10	open, close, or reversal that the live engine would observe.
    11	"""
    12	
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	import argparse
    16	import sys
    17	from pathlib import Path
    18	
    19	import numpy as np
    20	import pandas as pd
    21	
    22	sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
    23	import v15_m01_equity_reconstruct as m01  # noqa: E402
    24	from _streaming_io import ShardedParquetWriter, install_memory_guard  # noqa: E402
    25	
    26	
    27	EPS = 1e-9
    28	
    29	
    30	def classify_transition(before: float, after: float) -> str:
    31	    if abs(before) <= EPS and abs(after) <= EPS:
    32	        return "FLAT"
    33	    if abs(before) <= EPS:
    34	        return "ENTRY"
    35	    if abs(after) <= EPS:
    36	        return "EXIT"
    37	    if np.sign(before) != np.sign(after):
    38	        return "REVERSE"
    39	    if abs(after) > abs(before) + EPS:
    40	        return "ADDON"
    41	    if abs(after) < abs(before) - EPS:
    42	        return "TRIM"
    43	    return "NO_CHANGE"
    44	
    45	
    46	def build_atomic_actions(wallet: str, fills: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    47	    """Return one causally ordered action per perp fill, without leader equity.
    48	
    49	    ``startPosition`` is authoritative for the current transition.  A gap from
    50	    the prior observed position is nevertheless fail-closed for copy replay:
    51	    it proves that this event stream cannot reproduce what a stateful live
    52	    tracker would have known.  The current row reseeds state so later rows can
    53	    become valid again.
    54	    """
    55	    ordered = m01.order_wallet_fills_causally(list(fills))
    56	    prior_position: dict[str, float] = {}
    57	    rows: list[dict] = []
    58	    for fill in ordered:
    59	        coin = str(fill["coin"])
    60	        if not m01.coin_is_allowed_perp(coin):
    61	            continue
    62	        before = float(fill["startPosition"])
    63	        signed = float(fill["signed_sz"])
    64	        after = before + signed
    65	        price = float(fill["price"])
    66	        expected = prior_position.get(coin)
    67	        continuity_ok = expected is None or np.isclose(before, expected, rtol=1e-9, atol=1e-9)
    68	        order_ok = bool(fill.get("causal_order_ok", True))
    69	        values_ok = bool(
    70	            np.isfinite(before)
    71	            and np.isfinite(signed)
    72	            and np.isfinite(after)
    73	            and np.isfinite(price)
    74	            and price > 0
    75	            and abs(signed) > EPS
    76	        )
    77	        transition_valid = bool(order_ok and values_ok)
    78	        rows.append(
    79	            {
    80	                "wallet": wallet.lower(),
    81	                "coin": coin,
    82	                "ts": int(fill["time"]),
    83	                "fill_seq": int(fill.get("fill_seq", len(rows))),
    84	                "tid": fill.get("tid"),
    85	                "action_type": classify_transition(before, after),
    86	                "position_before": before,
    87	                "position_after": after,
    88	                "signed_size": signed,
    89	                "source_notional": abs(signed) * price,
    90	                "source_fee": float(fill.get("fee", 0.0) or 0.0),
    91	                "price": price,
    92	                # Mirrors V17's true-open dust convention at each source fill.
    93	                "live_open_candidate": bool(
    94	                    abs(before) * price < 1.0 and abs(after) * price >= 1.0
    95	                ),
    96	                "continuity_ok": bool(continuity_ok),
    97	                "causal_order_ok": order_ok,
    98	                "transition_valid": transition_valid,
    99	                # Strict stream-replay validity.  A historical startPosition
   100	                # can repair a gap; V17's raw websocket trade does not carry it.
   101	                "copy_signal_valid": bool(transition_valid and continuity_ok),
   102	                "state_resync": bool(expected is not None and not continuity_ok),
   103	            }
   104	        )
   105	        prior_position[coin] = after
   106	    return rows
   107	
   108	
   109	def build_burst_actions(wallet: str, fills: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   110	    """Net same-millisecond fills for diagnostics, not fidelity replay."""
   111	    ordered = m01.order_wallet_fills_causally(list(fills))
   112	    groups: dict[tuple[int, str], list[dict]] = {}
   113	    for fill in ordered:
   114	        if not m01.coin_is_allowed_perp(fill["coin"]):
   115	            continue
   116	        groups.setdefault((int(fill["time"]), str(fill["coin"])), []).append(fill)
   117	
   118	    prior_position: dict[str, float] = {}
   119	    rows: list[dict] = []
   120	    for (ts, coin), burst in sorted(groups.items()):
   121	        first, last = burst[0], burst[-1]
   122	        before = float(first["startPosition"])
   123	        after = float(last["startPosition"] + last["signed_sz"])
   124	        expected = prior_position.get(coin)
   125	        continuity_ok = expected is None or np.isclose(before, expected, rtol=1e-9, atol=1e-9)
   126	        order_ok = all(bool(f.get("causal_order_ok", True)) for f in burst)
   127	        total_signed = float(sum(float(f["signed_sz"]) for f in burst))
   128	        # A complete chain must reconcile its aggregate delta as an additional
   129	        # hard check; false means do not act on this burst.
   130	        aggregate_ok = np.isclose(after - before, total_signed, rtol=1e-9, atol=1e-9)
   131	        fee = float(sum(float(f.get("fee", 0.0) or 0.0) for f in burst))
   132	        notional = float(sum(abs(float(f["signed_sz"])) * float(f["price"]) for f in burst))
   133	        first_price = float(first["price"])
   134	        last_price = float(last["price"])
   135	        rows.append(
   136	            {
   137	                "wallet": wallet.lower(),
   138	                "coin": coin,
   139	                "ts": ts,
   140	                "action_type": classify_transition(before, after),
   141	                "position_before": before,
   142	                "position_after": after,
   143	                "signed_size": total_signed,
   144	                "n_source_fills": len(burst),
   145	                "source_notional": notional,
   146	                "source_fee": fee,
   147	                "first_price": first_price,
   148	                "last_price": last_price,
   149	                # Mirrors V17's true-open dust convention: pre-position below
   150	                # $1 notional, post-position at or above $1.
   151	                "live_open_candidate": bool(
   152	                    abs(before) * first_price < 1.0 and abs(after) * last_price >= 1.0
   153	                ),
   154	                "continuity_ok": bool(continuity_ok),
   155	                "causal_order_ok": bool(order_ok),
   156	                "aggregate_delta_ok": bool(aggregate_ok),
   157	                "transition_valid": bool(order_ok and aggregate_ok),
   158	                "copy_signal_valid": bool(order_ok and aggregate_ok and continuity_ok),
   159	                "state_resync": bool(expected is not None and not continuity_ok),
   160	            }
   161	        )
   162	        prior_position[coin] = after
   163	    return rows
   164	
   165	
   166	def main() -> None:
   167	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   168	    ap.add_argument("--wallets-file", type=Path, required=True)
   169	    ap.add_argument("--start", default="2025-12-01")
   170	    ap.add_argument("--end", default="2026-05-23")
   171	    ap.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
   172	    ap.add_argument("--memory-gb", type=float, default=3.0)
   173	    ap.add_argument(
   174	        "--granularity",
   175	        choices=("atomic", "burst"),
   176	        default="atomic",
   177	        help="atomic is required for live-fidelity replay; burst is diagnostic only",
   178	    )
   179	    args = ap.parse_args()
   180	    install_memory_guard(soft_gb=args.memory_gb, label="equity_independent_actions")
   181	    start_ms = int(pd.Timestamp(args.start, tz="UTC").timestamp() * 1000)
   182	    end_ms = int((pd.Timestamp(args.end, tz="UTC") + pd.Timedelta(days=1)).timestamp() * 1000 - 1)
   183	    wallets = [
   184	        line.strip().lower()
   185	        for line in args.wallets_file.read_text().splitlines()
   186	        if line.strip() and not line.startswith("#")
   187	    ]
   188	    writer = ShardedParquetWriter(args.output)
   189	    for wallet in wallets:
   190	        fills = m01.load_wallet_fills(wallet, start_ms, end_ms)
   191	        builder = build_atomic_actions if args.granularity == "atomic" else build_burst_actions
   192	        writer.add_many(builder(wallet, fills))
   193	    writer.close()
   194

   197	    main()

   183	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   184	# Worker-process globals (Mongo client + mark cache, lazily created per process)
   185	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   186	
   187	_mongo: Optional[pymongo.database.Database] = None
   188	_mark_cache: dict[tuple[str, int], Optional[float]] = {}
   189	# Per-coin in-memory 1m close series for ASOF lookups (replaces per-action Mongo round-trips, which
   190	# were ~94% of M2 wall time: 1.3M find_one calls / 10 wallets). Each coin loaded ONCE (one indexed
   191	# range query) into sorted (minutes, closes) numpy arrays; get_mark then does a binary-search asof.
   192	# Bounded memory: only coins actually touched, one series each. (Perf decision 2026-05-31.)
   193	_mark_series: dict[str, tuple] = {}
   194	
   195	
   196	def get_mongo() -> pymongo.database.Database:
   197	    global _mongo
   198	    if _mongo is None:
   199	        _mongo = pymongo.MongoClient(MONGO_URI)["quants_lab"]
   200	    return _mongo
   201	
   202	
   203	def get_mark(coin: str, ts_ms: int, causal: bool = False) -> Optional[float]:
   204	    """1m candle close at-or-before ts_ms (latest). None if no candle covers it.
   205	
   206	    Works for any prefix (main + xyz:/cash:/hyna:/para:/vntl:/flx:/km:) since the
   207	    candle ``coin`` carries the full prefixed symbol.
   208	
   209	    EXACT-MARK FIX (2026-06-04, Alberto "reconcile to the cent"): for EXOTIC HIP-3
   210	    coins (prefixed dexes xyz:/cash:/flx:/vntl:/km:/para:/hyna:) the trade-candle close
   211	    diverges materially from HL's setOracle mark (observed xyz:SILVER -12.8%, xyz:GOLD
   212	    -4.7% on 2026-03-04) -> on leveraged positions this is a dominant equity-recon drift
   213	    source. HL computes accountValue on the MARK (oracle), not the last trade. So we now
   214	    PREFER the oracle mark (hyperliquid_oracle, from v13_extract_oracle_marks setOracle)
   215	    for prefixed coins, with a candle fallback when oracle is absent/stale. Main-dex
   216	    (unprefixed) coins keep the trade-candle close (their oracle is consensus-level, not
   217	    in explorer_blocks; main mark precision tracked separately via asset_ctxs).
   218	
   219	    Set causal=True for forward decisions: use the last one-minute bar whose
   220	    close time is <= ts_ms, matching M07's convention (bar_open <= ts - 60s).
   221	    Keep causal=False for point-in-time MTM where the mark genuinely existed at ts_ms.
   222	    """
   223	    lookup_ms = (ts_ms // 60_000) * 60_000 - 60_000 if causal else ts_ms
   224	    # EXACT MARK first: exotic (prefixed) coins -> setOracle oracle; MAIN (unprefixed) coins ->
   225	    # asset_ctxs mark_px (HL's exact per-minute mark, the price accountValue uses). Candle fallback.
   226	    if ":" in coin:
   227	        # HL values accountValue/uPnL at the MARK price (markPxs[0]); ORACLE px is FUNDING-ONLY
   228	        # and is WRONG for m2m (robust-price-indices doc; Alberto, repeatedly). markPx ONLY here --
   229	        # NO oracle in the m2m path. If markPx is absent, fall through to the trade-candle last
   230	        # resort (still imperfect on thin books, but never the funding oracle).
   231	        mkpx = _markpx_lookup(coin, lookup_ms, ORACLE_MAX_AGE_MS)
   232	        if mkpx is not None:
   233	            return mkpx
   234	    else:
   235	        mpx = _assetctx_lookup(coin, lookup_ms, ASSETCTX_MAX_AGE_MS)
   236	        if mpx is not None:
   237	            return mpx
   238	    minute_key = lookup_ms // 60_000 * 60_000
   239	    mins, closes = _coin_series(coin)
   240	    if mins.size == 0:
   241	        return None
   242	    i = int(np.searchsorted(mins, minute_key, side="right")) - 1  # latest candle minute <= minute_key
   243	    if i < 0:
   244	        return None
   245	    # STALENESS CAP (codex marks-gate 2026-06-24): the candle fallback is at-or-before with NO age
   246	    # bound, so a candle from hours ago was returned as if current -> silently-stale MTM that callers
   247	    # treat as a live mark (markable_all=True). Cap the as-of age: if the nearest prior 1m bar is older
   248	    # than CANDLE_MAX_AGE_MS, return None so the caller flags it (degraded / NO_ANCHOR) instead of
   249	    # marking on a stale price. With dense all-market candles this rarely triggers; it only catches the
   250	    # genuinely-dormant-coin case the cap is meant to surface.
   251	    if minute_key - int(mins[i]) > CANDLE_MAX_AGE_MS:
   252	        return None
   253	    px = closes[i]
   254	    return None if px != px else float(px)  # NaN-safe (missing close -> None)
   255	
   256	
   257	# Candle-fallback staleness cap (codex marks-gate 2026-06-24). The 1m candle close is an at-or-before
   258	# lookup; without a bound a stale bar (delisted/dormant coin, or a data gap) is returned as a live mark.
   259	# Set to 15min to MATCH ASSETCTX_MAX_AGE_MS (codex recheck 2026-06-24: prefer 15min for a deploy-driving
   260	# reconstruction; 60min still allows materially-stale MTM on a fast-moving thin coin). With dense
   261	# all-market 1m candles a held coin has a bar most minutes, so this fires only on genuine dormancy/gaps
   262	# -> caller flags degraded/NO_ANCHOR. The pre/post-05-24 coverage+staleness AUDIT validates the cap does
   263	# not over-flag thin HIP-3 instruments; bump back toward 60min only if the audit shows false positives.
   264	CANDLE_MAX_AGE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000  # 15min (matches ASSETCTX_MAX_AGE_MS)
   265	
   266	# Equity-dependent selection requires repeated out-of-sample anchor checks.
   267	# One or zero segments is not enough evidence that the reconstruction is
   268	# stable; such wallets remain usable only in the equity-independent lane.
   269	MIN_INTER_ANCHOR_CHECKS = 2
   270	
   271	
   272	# Oracle/setOracle series, per-coin in-memory asof cache. ``mark_px`` is the
   273	# exact account-value mark when observed, but a sparse historical extraction is
   274	# not permission to reuse it for hours. The 30-wallet multi-dex sensitivity
   275	# showed 15 minutes weakly dominated 6 hours on inter-anchor drift (6 improved,
   276	# 0 worsened for median drift; 7 improved, 0 worsened for max drift). Older
   277	# observations therefore fall back to the contemporaneous trade candle.
   278	_oracle_series: dict[str, tuple] = {}
   279	ORACLE_MAX_AGE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000
   280	
   281	
   282	def _load_oracle_from_mongo(coin: str) -> tuple:
   283	    db = get_mongo()
   284	    cur = db.hyperliquid_oracle.find(
   285	        {"coin": coin}, projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "oracle_px": 1, "_id": 0}
   286	    )
   287	    pts: list[tuple[int, float]] = []
   288	    for d in cur:
   289	        t = d.get("timestamp_utc")
   290	        p = d.get("oracle_px")
   291	        if t is None or p is None:
   292	            continue
   293	        try:
   294	            pts.append((int(t), float(p)))
   295	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   296	            continue
   297	    pts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
   298	    if not pts:
   299	        return (np.empty(0, dtype="int64"), np.empty(0, dtype="float64"))
   300	    return (np.asarray([x[0] for x in pts], dtype="int64"),
   301	            np.asarray([x[1] for x in pts], dtype="float64"))
   302	
   303	
   304	def _oracle_lookup(coin: str, ts_ms: int, max_age_ms: int) -> Optional[float]:
   305	    s = _oracle_series.get(coin)
   306	    if s is None:
   307	        s = _load_oracle_from_mongo(coin)
   308	        _oracle_series[coin] = s
   309	    ts_arr, px = s
   310	    if ts_arr.size == 0:
   311	        return None
   312	    i = int(np.searchsorted(ts_arr, ts_ms, side="right")) - 1  # latest oracle point <= ts_ms
   313	    if i < 0:
   314	        return None
   315	    if ts_ms - int(ts_arr[i]) > max_age_ms:  # too stale -> let caller fall back to candle
   316	        return None
   317	    v = px[i]
   318	    return None if v != v else float(v)
   319	
   320	
   321	# setOracle MARK price (markPxs[0]) -- the price HL uses for accountValue/uPnL (oracle is funding-only).
   322	# Mirrors _oracle_lookup; reads the mark_px field written by scripts/pull_oracle_targeted.py.
   323	_markpx_series: dict[str, tuple] = {}
   324	
   325	
   326	def _load_markpx_from_mongo(coin: str) -> tuple:
   327	    db = get_mongo()
   328	    cur = db.hyperliquid_oracle.find(
   329	        {"coin": coin, "mark_px": {"$ne": None}},
   330	        projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "mark_px": 1, "_id": 0},
   331	    )
   332	    pts: list[tuple[int, float]] = []
   333	    for d in cur:
   334	        t = d.get("timestamp_utc")
   335	        p = d.get("mark_px")
   336	        if t is None or p is None:
   337	            continue
   338	        try:
   339	            pts.append((int(t), float(p)))
   340	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   341	            continue
   342	    pts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
   343	    if not pts:
   344	        return (np.empty(0, dtype="int64"), np.empty(0, dtype="float64"))
   345	    return (np.asarray([x[0] for x in pts], dtype="int64"),
   346	            np.asarray([x[1] for x in pts], dtype="float64"))
   347	
   348	
   349	def _markpx_lookup(coin: str, ts_ms: int, max_age_ms: int) -> Optional[float]:
   350	    s = _markpx_series.get(coin)
   351	    if s is None:
   352	        s = _load_markpx_from_mongo(coin)
   353	        _markpx_series[coin] = s
   354	    ts_arr, px = s
   355	    if ts_arr.size == 0:
   356	        return None
   357	    i = int(np.searchsorted(ts_arr, ts_ms, side="right")) - 1
   358	    if i < 0:
   359	        return None
   360	    if ts_ms - int(ts_arr[i]) > max_age_ms:
   361	        return None
   362	    v = px[i]
   363	    return None if v != v else float(v)
   364	
   365	
   366	# asset_ctxs EXACT mark for MAIN coins: per-coin (minute_ms, mark_px) .npy written by
   367	# scripts/extract_asset_ctx_marks.py. 1-min granularity -> tight staleness cap. Mirrors _coin_series.
   368	_assetctx_series: dict[str, tuple] = {}
   369	ASSETCTX_MAX_AGE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000  # 15min (asset_ctxs is 1-min; small cap)
   370	ASSETCTX_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "app" / "data" / "v15" / "assetctx_marks"
   371	
   372	
   373	def _assetctx_lookup(coin: str, ts_ms: int, max_age_ms: int) -> Optional[float]:
   374	    s = _assetctx_series.get(coin)
   375	    if s is None:
   376	        p = ASSETCTX_DIR / f"{_ulib.quote(coin, safe='')}.npy"
   377	        if p.exists():
   378	            try:
   379	                arr = np.load(p, mmap_mode="r")
   380	                s = (np.asarray(arr[0], dtype="int64"), np.asarray(arr[1], dtype="float64"))
   381	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
   382	                s = (np.empty(0, dtype="int64"), np.empty(0, dtype="float64"))
   383	        else:
   384	            s = (np.empty(0, dtype="int64"), np.empty(0, dtype="float64"))
   385	        _assetctx_series[coin] = s
   386	    ts_arr, px = s
   387	    if ts_arr.size == 0:
   388	        return None
   389	    i = int(np.searchsorted(ts_arr, ts_ms, side="right")) - 1
   390	    if i < 0:
   391	        return None
   392	    if ts_ms - int(ts_arr[i]) > max_age_ms:
   393	        return None
   394	    v = px[i]
   395	    return None if v != v else float(v)
   396	
   397	
   398	# Earliest ms at which a coin has ANY price (candle minute, else oracle ts). A coin cannot be
   399	# held before it has a price (its listing) -> used to bound phantom position back-projection in
   400	# seed_positions. None = no price source ever. Cached.
   401	_coin_first_price: dict[str, Optional[int]] = {}
   402	
   403	
   404	def _coin_first_price_ms(coin: str) -> Optional[int]:
   405	    if coin in _coin_first_price:
   406	        return _coin_first_price[coin]
   407	    fp: Optional[int] = None
   408	    mins, _ = _coin_series(coin)
   409	    if mins.size:
   410	        fp = int(mins[0])
   411	    if fp is None:  # no candle -> try oracle series
   412	        oser = _oracle_series.get(coin)
   413	        if oser is None:
   414	            oser = _load_oracle_from_mongo(coin)
   415	            _oracle_series[coin] = oser
   416	        ts_arr, _ = oser
   417	        if ts_arr.size:
   418	            fp = int(ts_arr[0])
   419	    _coin_first_price[coin] = fp
   420	    return fp
   421

   423	import urllib.parse as _ulib
   424	
   425	MARKS_CACHE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "app" / "data" / "v15" / "marks_cache"
   426	
   427	
   428	def _coin_cache_path(coin: str) -> Path:
   429	    return MARKS_CACHE_DIR / f"{_ulib.quote(coin, safe='')}.npy"
   430	
   431	
   432	def _load_coin_from_mongo(coin: str) -> tuple:
   433	    db = get_mongo()
   434	    cur = db.hyperliquid_candles.find(
   435	        {"coin": coin, "interval": "1m"},
   436	        projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "close": 1, "_id": 0},
   437	    ).sort("timestamp_utc", 1)
   438	    mins_l: list[int] = []
   439	    closes_l: list[float] = []
   440	    for d in cur:
   441	        t = d.get("timestamp_utc")
   442	        if t is None:
   443	            continue
   444	        c = d.get("close")
   445	        mins_l.append(int(t))
   446	        closes_l.append(float(c) if c is not None else float("nan"))
   447	    return (np.asarray(mins_l, dtype="int64"), np.asarray(closes_l, dtype="float64"))
   448	
   449	
   450	def _marks_manifest_path(out_dir: Path) -> Path:
   451	    return out_dir / "_manifest.json"
   452	
   453	
   454	def marks_cache_status(out_dir: Path | None = None) -> dict:
   455	    """Cheap freshness check: compares the cached coin-set against Mongo's current 1m coin-set + the
   456	    latest 1m candle minute (indexed). Returns {fresh, age_days, reason}. (codex perf-r1 #3)"""
   457	    import json as _json
   458	    out_dir = Path(out_dir) if out_dir else MARKS_CACHE_DIR
   459	    mp = _marks_manifest_path(out_dir)
   460	    if not mp.exists():
   461	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": None, "reason": "no manifest"}
   462	    man = _json.loads(mp.read_text())
   463	    db = get_mongo()
   464	    cur_coins = set(db.hyperliquid_candles.distinct("coin", {"interval": "1m"}))
   465	    # latest 1m candle minute overall (index-backed via the coin+interval+ts index is per-coin; use a
   466	    # bounded find sorted desc which the planner serves from the compound index).
   467	    latest = db.hyperliquid_candles.find_one({"interval": "1m"}, sort=[("timestamp_utc", -1)],
   468	                                             projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "_id": 0})
   469	    cur_max = int(latest["timestamp_utc"]) if latest else 0
   470	    cur_total = int(db.hyperliquid_candles.estimated_document_count())  # O(1) metadata; catches backfills
   471	    import time as _t
   472	    age_days = (_t.time() - man.get("built_unix", 0)) / 86400.0
   473	    if set(man.get("coins", [])) != cur_coins:
   474	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": age_days, "reason": f"coin set changed ({len(cur_coins)} now vs {len(man.get('coins', []))} cached)"}
   475	    if cur_max > int(man.get("max_minute", 0)):
   476	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": age_days, "reason": f"new candles since build (max {cur_max} > {man.get('max_minute')})"}
   477	    # codex perf-r2 #3: total-count signature catches INTERIOR backfills (gap fills / corrections that
   478	    # add docs) that do NOT advance the global max minute. (In-place value overwrites that preserve
   479	    # doc count remain undetectable cheaply -> use --rebuild-marks-cache after any such correction.)
   480	    if "total_candles" not in man:
   481	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": age_days, "reason": "manifest missing total_candles; rebuild required"}
   482	    if cur_total != int(man["total_candles"]):
   483	        return {"fresh": False, "age_days": age_days, "reason": f"candle count changed ({cur_total} vs {man['total_candles']}) -> possible backfill"}
   484	    return {"fresh": True, "age_days": age_days, "reason": "coin-set + max-minute + count match"}
   485	
   486	
   487	def build_marks_cache(out_dir: Path | None = None, force: bool = False) -> int:
   488	    """PRECOMPUTE ONCE (single process, one Mongo pass per coin): write each coin's 1m (minute,close)
   489	    series to a local .npy so the worker pool reads marks from local files instead of each of N
   490	    workers re-scanning Mongo (the 74%-idle I/O stall). Returns #coins written. Idempotent: skips
   491	    coins already cached unless force. Writes a manifest for freshness validation. Unique temp names
   492	    so concurrent builds never clobber. (Perf decision 2026-05-31; mandatory streaming-io sibling.)"""
   493	    import json as _json
   494	    import time as _t
   495	    out_dir = Path(out_dir) if out_dir else MARKS_CACHE_DIR
   496	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   497	    db = get_mongo()
   498	    coins = db.hyperliquid_candles.distinct("coin", {"interval": "1m"})
   499	    n = 0
   500	    max_minute = 0
   501	    for coin in coins:
   502	        p = out_dir / f"{_ulib.quote(coin, safe='')}.npy"
   503	        if p.exists() and not force:
   504	            continue
   505	        mins, closes = _load_coin_from_mongo(coin)
   506	        if mins.size:
   507	            max_minute = max(max_minute, int(mins[-1]))
   508	        arr = np.vstack([mins.astype("float64"), closes]) if mins.size else np.empty((2, 0), dtype="float64")
   509	        # codex perf-r1 #4: unique tmp (pid) so concurrent builds/runs never race on one tmp name.
   510	        tmp = p.with_name(f"{p.name}.{os.getpid()}.tmp")
   511	        with open(tmp, "wb") as fh:   # file handle: np.save must NOT re-append .npy to the name
   512	            np.save(fh, arr)
   513	        tmp.replace(p)
   514	        n += 1
   515	    # manifest: record the full coin set + the latest candle minute observed (freshness signature).
   516	    latest = db.hyperliquid_candles.find_one({"interval": "1m"}, sort=[("timestamp_utc", -1)],
   517	                                             projection={"timestamp_utc": 1, "_id": 0})
   518	    cur_max = int(latest["timestamp_utc"]) if latest else max_minute
   519	    _marks_manifest_path(out_dir).write_text(_json.dumps({
   520	        "built_unix": _t.time(), "coins": list(coins), "n_coins": len(coins), "max_minute": cur_max,
   521	        "total_candles": int(db.hyperliquid_candles.estimated_document_count()),
   522	    }))
   523	    logger.info(f"build_marks_cache: wrote {n} coin series to {out_dir} ({len(coins)} coins total)")
   524	    return n
   525	
   526	
   527	def _coin_series(coin: str) -> tuple:
   528	    """Lazily load+cache a coin's FULL 1m (minute, close) series as sorted numpy arrays. Prefers the
   529	    local precomputed .npy (page-cached, shared across workers, NO Mongo); falls back to a one-shot
   530	    Mongo range query if the cache file is absent. Equivalent to the prior per-call
   531	    `timestamp_utc <= minute_key, sort desc, limit 1` lookup (proven byte-identical in validation)."""
   532	    s = _mark_series.get(coin)
   533	    if s is not None:
   534	        return s
   535	    p = _coin_cache_path(coin)
   536	    if p.exists():
   537	        try:
   538	            arr = np.load(p, mmap_mode="r")
   539	            s = (np.asarray(arr[0], dtype="int64"), np.asarray(arr[1], dtype="float64"))
   540	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001  corrupt cache -> Mongo fallback
   541	            s = _load_coin_from_mongo(coin)
   542	    else:
   543	        s = _load_coin_from_mongo(coin)
   544	    _mark_series[coin] = s
   545	    return s
   546

   552	
   553	def coin_is_spot(coin: str) -> bool:
   554	    """Return true for non-perp trade assets excluded from reconstruction.
   555	
   556	    ``@`` and slash-delimited symbols denote spot pairs; ``#`` denotes HIP-4
   557	    outcome markets. Outcome fills have Buy/Sell/Settlement semantics, not perp
   558	    position semantics.
   559	    """
   560	    return coin.startswith(("@", "#")) or "/" in coin or coin == "USDC"
   561	
   562	
   563	def coin_is_dropped(coin: str) -> bool:
   564	    """Return whether a configured perp-dex prefix is excluded."""
   565	    return any(coin.startswith(p) for p in DROPPED_DEX_PREFIXES)
   566	
   567	
   568	def coin_dex(coin: str) -> str:
   569	    """Return the perp dex name for a coin. Unprefixed -> 'main'."""
   570	    if ":" in coin:
   571	        return coin.split(":", 1)[0]
   572	    return "main"
   573	
   574	
   575	# ALL DEXES IN SCOPE (Alberto 2026-05-30: "ALL dexes become IN SCOPE").
   576	# The only equity history HL gives is whole-account perpAllTime (the portfolio
   577	# `dex` param is IGNORED -> verified 2026-05-30, it returns the same whole-account
   578	# number for dex=main/xyz/default). So reconstructing EVERY perp dex makes our
   579	# equity match the anchor instead of fighting it. Any perp coin (any "<dex>:"
   580	# prefix, incl flx:, or unprefixed main) is in scope; spot (@-tokens,
   581	# slash-delimited pairs, USDC) and HIP-4 outcome markets (#-tokens) are excluded.
   582	# (USDC capital-flow dex scoping is handled by dex_in_scope(): any non-spot dex.)
   583	# Dexes with a row in wallet_anchor_state.parquet (position seed source). main +
   584	# xyz + flx are seedable from the parquet; cash/hyna/para/vntl seed from fills
   585	# only (still IN SCOPE, just no static-anchor seed -> rely on fills + drift gate).
   586	ANCHOR_COVERED_DEXES = frozenset({"main", "xyz", "flx"})
   587	
   588	
   589	def coin_is_allowed_perp(coin: str) -> bool:
   590	    """True for any in-scope perp; excludes spot and HIP-4 outcomes."""
   591	    return not coin_is_spot(coin)
   592	
   593	
   594	def dex_in_scope(dex: str) -> bool:
   595	    """A dex name (coin_dex output / ledger sourceDex) is in scope if it is any
   596	    perp dex. Spot markers are out. All perp dexes are in scope."""
   597	    d = (dex or "").strip().lower()
   598	    return d not in ("spot", "@")
