  1932	
  1933	def position_validation(
  1934	    stream, fills, anchor: AnchorState, wallet_lc, window_anchors
  1935	) -> dict:
  1936	    """Accuracy #4: reconstructed per-coin positions AT the anchor-parquet fetch
  1937	    time vs the parquet positions_json (main+xyz). Both reference the SAME
  1938	    timestamp (anchor.fetched_ms), so the extended fill stream (loaded to
  1939	    fetched_ms) is required — positions are mark-independent.
  1940	
  1941	    Reports per-coin mismatch count (rel error > 1% AND abs error > 1e-6) plus
  1942	    the worst absolute size error for diagnostics.
  1943	    """
  1944	    if not window_anchors:
  1945	        return {
  1946	            "n_position_mismatches": np.nan,
  1947	            "n_positions_checked": 0,
  1948	            "max_position_abs_err": np.nan,
  1949	            "position_validation_status": "no_anchor",
  1950	        }
  1951	    # The anchor parquet snapshots positions at anchor.fetched_ms. Our local S3
  1952	    # fills may end BEFORE fetched_ms (the daily parquets lag the anchor pull).
  1953	    # If so, reconstructed positions reflect an earlier moment than the parquet
  1954	    # snapshot and the comparison is cross-time / not meaningful. Detect and flag.
  1955	    last_fill_ms = max((f["time"] for f in fills), default=0)
  1956	    gap_h = (anchor.fetched_ms - last_fill_ms) / 3_600_000 if last_fill_ms else float("inf")
  1957	    if gap_h > 24:
  1958	        return {
  1959	            "n_position_mismatches": np.nan,
  1960	            "n_positions_checked": 0,
  1961	            "max_position_abs_err": np.nan,
  1962	            "position_validation_status": f"fills_end_{gap_h:.0f}h_before_anchor",
  1963	        }
  1964	    a_t, a_v = window_anchors[-1]
  1965	    wr = compute_eq_at(stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, anchor.fetched_ms, a_t, a_v)
  1966	    recon = wr.positions
  1967	    mismatches = 0
  1968	    checked = 0
  1969	    max_abs_err = 0.0
  1970	    coins = set(recon) | set(anchor.positions)
  1971	    for c in coins:
  1972	        if coin_is_dropped(c) or coin_is_spot(c):
  1973	            continue
  1974	        r = recon.get(c, 0.0)
  1975	        a = float(anchor.positions.get(c, 0.0))
  1976	        checked += 1
  1977	        err = abs(r - a)
  1978	        max_abs_err = max(max_abs_err, err)
  1979	        denom = max(abs(a), abs(r), 1e-6)
  1980	        if err > 1e-6 and err / denom > 0.01:
  1981	            mismatches += 1
  1982	    return {
  1983	        "n_position_mismatches": mismatches,
  1984	        "n_positions_checked": checked,
  1985	        "max_position_abs_err": max_abs_err,
  1986	        "position_validation_status": "ok",
  1987	    }
  1988	
  1989	
  1990	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1991	# M02 BRIDGE — additive per-event equity series (NO reconstruction-MATH change)
  1992	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
  1993	#
  1994	# This block is ADDITIVE for V15 M02 (journey_trace). It does NOT touch the
  1995	# existing daily/audit reconstruction above; it reuses the SAME helpers
  1996	# (seed_positions, get_mark, fill/ledger/funding cash deltas) to emit, per
  1997	# wallet, a PER-EVENT equity_post series in the TOTAL EVENT ORDER required by
  1998	# the M02 design spec:
  1999	#
  2000	#   event_order key = (ts_ms, type_rank, source_seq)
  2001	#     type_rank: ledger=0, fill=1, funding=2   (ledger settles, then trade,
  2002	#                then funding accrues on the post-trade position)
  2003	#     source_seq: stable causal fill sequence; per-type append index otherwise.
  2004	#
  2005	# For each event k we re-anchor to the last weekly anchor with anchor_ts
  2006	# STRICTLY < ts(k) (codex r3: an anchor sharing the action's ms is NOT a valid
  2007	# base), walk forward applying every event with order <= k (incl. this one),
  2008	# then mark-to-market at ts(k). equity_post(k) therefore uses ONLY data with
  2009	# order <= k — no look-ahead. We also emit the per-event reliability fields M02
  2010	# needs to pick its equity-basis mode (markable_all, n_unmarkable, frozen
  2011	# component value/age, age_since_anchor, mark_ts, anchor_ts) — all derived from
  2012	# data <= k only.
  2013	
  2014	# type_rank for the total event order (codex r3/r4).
  2015	_TYPE_RANK = {"ledger": 0, "fill": 1, "funding": 2}
  2016	
  2017	# STALE_CAP: fall back if reconstruction base anchor is older than one anchor
  2018	# cycle (7d) while material unmarkable exposure exists. Tunable (spec).
  2019	STALE_CAP_MS = 7 * 86_400_000
  2020	
  2021	SPOT_USDC_INVARIANT_NOTE = (
  2022	    "HL is a unified wallet: spot USDC IS perp margin. The weekly perpAllTime "
  2023	    "anchor (marginSummary.accountValue summed across perp dexes) already "
  2024	    "includes collateral (USDC) + uPnL. Re-anchoring snaps cash := anchor_eq - "
  2025	    "Sum pos*mark, so the spot-USDC/collateral component is captured inside "
  2026	    "`cash` and carried causally by ledger deposit/withdraw/transfer deltas. "
  2027	    "equity_post is therefore whole-account (collateral-inclusive) by "
  2028	    "construction; see test_m01_spot_collateral_invariant."
  2029	)
  2030	
  2031	
  2032	def build_event_stream(
  2033	    fills: list[dict],
  2034	    funding: list[dict],
  2035	    ledger: list[dict],
  2036	) -> list[dict]:
  2037	    """Merge fills+funding+ledger into the TOTAL EVENT ORDER (spec v4).
  2038	
  2039	    Returns a list of event dicts each carrying:
  2040	        ts, type ('fill'|'funding'|'ledger'), type_rank, source_seq, ev (payload)
  2041	    sorted by (ts, type_rank, source_seq). source_seq is the causal per-wallet
  2042	    fill sequence, else a stable per-type append index so the order is deterministic
  2043	    and reproducible across M01 (equity) and M02 (actions).
  2044	
  2045	    SAME-MS FILL ORDER: tid is identity, not sequence. Partial fills are ordered
  2046	    by chaining startPosition -> startPosition+signed_size. The fill payload is
  2047	    carried directly so M2 never rematches on a lossy key.
  2048	    """
  2049	    out: list[dict] = []
  2050	    ordered_fills = order_wallet_fills_causally(list(fills))
  2051	    for i, f in enumerate(ordered_fills):
  2052	        if not coin_is_allowed_perp(f["coin"]):
  2053	            continue
  2054	        seq = int(f.get("fill_seq", i))
  2055	        out.append(
  2056	            {
  2057	                "ts": int(f["time"]),
  2058	                "type": "fill",
  2059	                "type_rank": _TYPE_RANK["fill"],
  2060	                "source_seq": seq,
  2061	                "ev": f,
  2062	            }
  2063	        )
  2064	    for i, e in enumerate(ledger):
  2065	        out.append(
  2066	            {
  2067	                "ts": int(e["time"]),
  2068	                "type": "ledger",
  2069	                "type_rank": _TYPE_RANK["ledger"],
  2070	                "source_seq": i,
  2071	                "ev": e,
  2072	            }
  2073	        )
  2074	    for i, e in enumerate(funding):
  2075	        out.append(
  2076	            {
  2077	                "ts": int(e["time"]),
  2078	                "type": "funding",
  2079	                "type_rank": _TYPE_RANK["funding"],
  2080	                "source_seq": i,
  2081	                "ev": e,
  2082	            }
  2083	        )
  2084	    out.sort(key=lambda x: (x["ts"], x["type_rank"], x["source_seq"]))
  2085	    return out
  2086	
  2087	
  2088	@dataclass
  2089	class EventEquity:
  2090	    """Per-event reconstruction output (whole-account, causal)."""
  2091	
  2092	    event_order: int           # strictly monotone index in the ordered stream
  2093	    ts: int
  2094	    type: str
  2095	    source_seq: int
  2096	    equity_post: float         # whole-account equity AFTER this event
  2097	    cash: float
  2098	    position_value: float
  2099	    anchor_ts: Optional[int]   # base anchor (strictly < ts); None if no past anchor
  2100	    anchor_equity: Optional[float]
  2101	    age_since_anchor_ms: Optional[int]
  2102	    mark_ts: Optional[int]     # latest mark <= ts across marked positions
  2103	    markable_all: bool         # every held nonzero position markable at ts
  2104	    n_unmarkable: int
  2105	    no_extradex_without_anchor: bool
  2106	    frozen_component_value: float   # last-known value of unmarkable positions
  2107	    frozen_component_age_ms: int    # staleness of the frozen value at ts
  2108	    has_past_anchor: bool
  2109	    fill: Optional[dict] = None     # for type=='fill': the EXACT fill payload of
  2110	                                    # this event (codex r4 BUG 2) — M02 consumes it
  2111	                                    # directly off the stream, never rematching by
  2112	                                    # (ts, tid). None for funding/ledger events.
  2113	
  2114	
  2115	def compute_event_equity(
  2116	    ordered: list[dict],
  2117	    fills: list[dict],
  2118	    anchor: "AnchorState",
  2119	    wallet_lc: str,
  2120	    window_anchors: list[tuple[int, float]],
  2121	    extradex_no_anchor: bool,
  2122	    causal_mark: bool = True,
  2123	) -> list[EventEquity]:
  2124	    """Walk the ordered event stream ONCE and emit equity_post per event.
  2125	
  2126	    Causal contract (spec NON-LOOK-AHEAD INVARIANTS):
  2127	      * base anchor for event k = last weekly anchor with anchor_ts STRICTLY < ts(k).
  2128	      * equity_post(k) = cash + Sum pos*mark(ts(k)) using ONLY events order <= k.
  2129	        By default the terminal live-position MTM uses closed causal marks so
  2130	        M02's sizing denominator matches its causal numerator.
  2131	      * mark_ts <= ts(k); anchor_ts < ts(k); event_order strictly monotone.
  2132	
  2133	    We re-seed/re-snap cash whenever the active base anchor changes (each weekly
  2134	    anchor cycle), exactly like compute_eq_at, then incrementally apply each
  2135	    event's cash/position delta. Mark-to-market is recomputed at each event ts
  2136	    over the live positions, tracking unmarkable positions' last-known value
  2137	    (the FROZEN component) so M02 can do PARTIAL_MTM without look-ahead.
  2138	    """
  2139	    results: list[EventEquity] = []
  2140	
  2141	    # Active base-anchor state. Re-initialised lazily when the base anchor for
  2142	    # the current event differs from the one in force.
  2143	    active_anchor_ts: Optional[int] = None
  2144	    active_anchor_eq: Optional[float] = None
  2145	    cash = 0.0
  2146	    positions: dict[str, float] = {}
  2147	    # last-known mark value per coin (for the frozen unmarkable component)
  2148	    last_val: dict[str, float] = {}
  2149	    last_val_ts: dict[str, int] = {}
  2150	    # Coins whose carry-in (pre-anchor holding) has already been folded into the
  2151	    # book+cash under the ACTIVE anchor. Reset per anchor cycle (codex r4 BUG 1).
  2152	    carried_in: set[str] = set()
  2153	
  2154	    def _carry_in_fill(ev: dict) -> None:
  2155	        """CAUSAL replacement for seed_positions case 2 (codex r4 BUG 1).
  2156	
  2157	        seed_positions(causal_cutoff=True) deliberately omits a coin first traded
  2158	        AFTER the active anchor, to avoid look-ahead at earlier events. But that
  2159	        coin DID hold a pre-anchor position (its first fill's startPosition), which
  2160	        the anchor equity a_v already includes. The first time we REACH that coin's
  2161	        fill in the ordered walk (order <= k = causal), we fold its carry-in in the
  2162	        SAME way case 2 + the cash snap would have: add startPosition to the book
  2163	        AND subtract its anchor-time value from cash (mark at active_anchor_ts < ts,
  2164	        causal) so equity is not double-counted. Coins already in the anchor seed,
  2165	        or already folded, are skipped. Identical end-state to compute_eq_at, but
  2166	        revealed only at the causal moment instead of at the anchor (no leak at
  2167	        earlier events)."""
  2168	        nonlocal cash
  2169	        coin = ev["coin"]
  2170	        if coin in carried_in or active_anchor_ts is None:
  2171	            return
  2172	        carried_in.add(coin)
  2173	        sp = float(ev.get("startPosition", 0.0) or 0.0)
  2174	        if abs(sp) <= 1e-9:
  2175	            return
  2176	        positions[coin] = positions.get(coin, 0.0) + sp
  2177	        m = get_mark(coin, active_anchor_ts)
  2178	        if m is not None:
  2179	            cash -= sp * m  # mirror the anchor cash snap for this carried-in coin
  2180	            last_val[coin] = sp * m
  2181	            last_val_ts.setdefault(coin, active_anchor_ts)
  2182	
  2183	    def _past_anchor(ts: int) -> Optional[tuple[int, float]]:
  2184	        chosen = None
  2185	        for a_t, a_v in window_anchors:
  2186	            if a_t < ts:            # STRICT (codex r3)
  2187	                chosen = (a_t, a_v)
  2188	            else:
  2189	                break
  2190	        return chosen
  2191	
  2192	    for order, item in enumerate(ordered):
  2193	        ts = item["ts"]
  2194	        base = _past_anchor(ts)
  2195	
  2196	        if base is None:
  2197	            # No past anchor → cannot reconstruct a causal denominator. Still
  2198	            # advance the position book from startPosition-derived seeding so
  2199	            # that once an anchor appears the book is correct, but emit
  2200	            # has_past_anchor=False (M02 maps this to NO_ANCHOR).
  2201	            # We seed positions at this ts from fills only (causal: positions_at
  2202	            # uses startPosition + cumulative signed_sz, all <= ts).
  2203	            ev = item["ev"]
  2204	            if item["type"] == "fill":
  2205	                positions[ev["coin"]] = positions.get(ev["coin"], 0.0) + ev["signed_sz"]
  2206	                if abs(positions[ev["coin"]]) < 1e-9:
  2207	                    positions.pop(ev["coin"], None)
  2208	            results.append(
  2209	                EventEquity(
  2210	                    event_order=order, ts=ts, type=item["type"],
  2211	                    source_seq=item["source_seq"], equity_post=float("nan"),
  2212	                    cash=float("nan"), position_value=float("nan"),
  2213	                    anchor_ts=None, anchor_equity=None, age_since_anchor_ms=None,
  2214	                    mark_ts=None, markable_all=False, n_unmarkable=0,
  2215	                    no_extradex_without_anchor=(not extradex_no_anchor),
  2216	                    frozen_component_value=0.0, frozen_component_age_ms=0,
  2217	                    has_past_anchor=False,
  2218	                    fill=(item["ev"] if item["type"] == "fill" else None),
  2219	                )
  2220	            )
  2221	            continue
  2222	
  2223	        a_t, a_v = base
  2224	        if a_t != active_anchor_ts:
  2225	            # New base anchor cycle → re-seed positions at the anchor and snap
  2226	            # cash, identically to compute_eq_at. This rebuild uses only fills
  2227	            # (startPosition + cumulative) at/<= anchor_ts, which is < ts → causal.
  2228	            active_anchor_ts = a_t
  2229	            active_anchor_eq = a_v
  2230	            seeded = seed_positions(fills, anchor, a_t, causal_cutoff=True)
  2231	            anchor_pos_value = 0.0
  2232	            for c, sz in seeded.items():
  2233	                if abs(sz) < 1e-9:
  2234	                    continue
  2235	                m = get_mark(c, a_t)
  2236	                if m is not None:
  2237	                    anchor_pos_value += sz * m
  2238	                    last_val[c] = sz * m
  2239	                    last_val_ts[c] = a_t
  2240	            cash = a_v - anchor_pos_value
  2241	            positions = {c: s for c, s in seeded.items() if abs(s) > 1e-9}
  2242	            # Coins already in the anchor seed are carried-in; their fills must NOT
  2243	            # re-fold a startPosition. Coins NOT seeded (first traded after anchor)
  2244	            # get folded causally at their first fill via _carry_in_fill.
  2245	            carried_in = set(seeded.keys())
  2246	            # Replay events strictly between the anchor and this event (anchor_ts
  2247	            # < e_ts < ts) so the book/cash reflect everything order <= current.
  2248	            for prev in ordered:
  2249	                p_ts = prev["ts"]
  2250	                if p_ts <= a_t:
  2251	                    continue
  2252	                # stop once we reach the current event's order position
  2253	                if (p_ts, prev["type_rank"], prev["source_seq"]) >= (
  2254	                    ts, item["type_rank"], item["source_seq"]
  2255	                ):
  2256	                    break
  2257	                if prev["type"] == "fill":
  2258	                    _carry_in_fill(prev["ev"])
  2259	                _apply(prev, positions, last_val, last_val_ts, _cash_ref=None)
  2260	                cash = _apply_cash(prev, cash, wallet_lc)
  2261	
  2262	        # Apply THIS event (order == k included).
  2263	        if item["type"] == "fill":
  2264	            _carry_in_fill(item["ev"])
  2265	        _apply(item, positions, last_val, last_val_ts, _cash_ref=None)
  2266	        cash = _apply_cash(item, cash, wallet_lc)
  2267	
  2268	        # Mark-to-market at ts over live positions; track frozen component.
  2269	        pos_value = 0.0
  2270	        n_unmarkable = 0
  2271	        mark_ts_latest: Optional[int] = None
  2272	        frozen_value = 0.0
  2273	        frozen_age = 0
  2274	        for c, sz in positions.items():
  2275	            if abs(sz) < 1e-9:
  2276	                continue
  2277	            m = get_mark(c, ts, causal=causal_mark)
  2278	            if m is None:
  2279	                n_unmarkable += 1
  2280	                fv = last_val.get(c, 0.0)
  2281	                frozen_value += fv
  2282	                if c in last_val_ts:
  2283	                    frozen_age = max(frozen_age, ts - last_val_ts[c])
  2284	                continue
  2285	            val = sz * m
  2286	            pos_value += val
  2287	            last_val[c] = val
  2288	            mk = ts // 60_000 * 60_000 - (60_000 if causal_mark else 0)
  2289	            last_val_ts[c] = mk
  2290	            mark_ts_latest = mk if mark_ts_latest is None else max(mark_ts_latest, mk)
  2291	
  2292	        equity_post = cash + pos_value  # markable + cash; frozen excluded here
  2293	        results.append(
  2294	            EventEquity(
  2295	                event_order=order, ts=ts, type=item["type"],
  2296	                source_seq=item["source_seq"],
  2297	                equity_post=equity_post + frozen_value,  # whole-account incl frozen
  2298	                cash=cash, position_value=pos_value,
  2299	                anchor_ts=a_t, anchor_equity=a_v,
  2300	                age_since_anchor_ms=ts - a_t,
  2301	                mark_ts=mark_ts_latest if mark_ts_latest is not None else (ts if not positions else None),
  2302	                markable_all=(n_unmarkable == 0),
  2303	                n_unmarkable=n_unmarkable,
  2304	                no_extradex_without_anchor=(not extradex_no_anchor),
  2305	                frozen_component_value=frozen_value,
  2306	                frozen_component_age_ms=frozen_age,
  2307	                has_past_anchor=True,
  2308	                fill=(item["ev"] if item["type"] == "fill" else None),
  2309	            )
  2310	        )
  2311	    return results
  2312	
  2313	
  2314	def _apply(item: dict, positions: dict, last_val: dict, last_val_ts: dict, _cash_ref=None) -> None:
  2315	    """Apply an event's POSITION delta (cash handled by _apply_cash)."""
  2316	    if item["type"] == "fill":
  2317	        ev = item["ev"]
  2318	        coin = ev["coin"]
  2319	        positions[coin] = positions.get(coin, 0.0) + ev["signed_sz"]
  2320	        if abs(positions[coin]) < 1e-9:
  2321	            positions.pop(coin, None)
  2322	
  2323	
  2324	def _apply_cash(item: dict, cash: float, wallet_lc: str) -> float:
  2325	    """Apply an event's CASH delta (mirrors compute_eq_at's walk)."""
  2326	    t = item["type"]
  2327	    ev = item["ev"]
  2328	    if t == "fill":
  2329	        return cash + fill_cash_delta(ev)
  2330	    if t == "ledger":
  2331	        return cash + ledger_cash_delta(ev, wallet_lc).cash
  2332	    if t == "funding":
  2333	        return cash + funding_cash_delta(ev)
  2334	    return cash
  2335	
  2336	
  2337	def reconstruct_wallet_event_equity(args: tuple) -> dict:
  2338	    """M02 bridge driver: emit the per-event equity series + anchors for one wallet.
  2339	
  2340	    Returns {'wallet', 'events': [EventEquity...], 'weekly_anchors': [(ts,eq)],
  2341	    'fills': [...], 'funding': [...], 'inter_anchor_drift': {...} (diagnostic
  2342	    only, NEVER used to switch modes), 'extradex_no_anchor': bool}.
  2343	    Mirrors reconstruct_wallet's loading + ordering exactly so equity_post is
  2344	    consistent with the audited daily reconstruction.
  2345	    """
  2346	    wallet, anchor, start_ms, end_ms = args
  2347	    wallet_lc = wallet.lower()
  2348	    if anchor is None:
  2349	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_anchor"}
  2350	
  2351	    avh = get_portfolio_perp(wallet)
  2352	    valid_anchors = [(t, v) for t, v in avh if v > 0.01]
  2353	    if not valid_anchors:
  2354	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_valid_anchors"}
  2355	
  2356	    walk_end_ms = min(end_ms, anchor.fetched_ms)
  2357	    load_end_ms = max(walk_end_ms, int(anchor.fetched_ms))
  2358	
  2359	    fills = load_wallet_fills(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  2360	    funding = load_wallet_funding(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  2361	    ledger = load_wallet_ledger(wallet, start_ms, load_end_ms)
  2362	
  2363	    window_anchors = [(t, v) for t, v in valid_anchors if start_ms <= t <= walk_end_ms]
  2364	    if not window_anchors:
  2365	        return {"wallet": wallet, "error": "no_window_anchors"}
  2366	
  2367	    source_dexes = sorted({coin_dex(f["coin"]) for f in fills} | {
  2368	        coin_dex(c) for c in anchor.positions
  2369	    })
  2370	    extradex_no_anchor = bool([
  2371	        d for d in source_dexes
  2372	        if dex_in_scope(d) and d not in ANCHOR_COVERED_DEXES
  2373	    ])
  2374	
  2375	    ordered = build_event_stream(fills, funding, ledger)
  2376	    # Only emit events up to walk_end_ms (mark coverage); load_end may exceed it
  2377	    # but those tail events have no marks and are not sizing-relevant for M02.
  2378	    ordered = [e for e in ordered if e["ts"] <= walk_end_ms]
  2379	
  2380	    events = compute_event_equity(
  2381	        ordered, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, window_anchors, extradex_no_anchor
  2382	    )
  2383	
  2384	    # diagnostic only (never a mode switch): reuse the existing M01 drift calc.
  2385	    stream = [(e["ts"], e["type"], e["ev"]) for e in ordered]
  2386	    inter = inter_anchor_drift(stream, fills, anchor, wallet_lc, window_anchors)
  2387	
  2388	    return {
  2389	        "wallet": wallet,
  2390	        "events": events,
  2391	        "weekly_anchors": window_anchors,
  2392	        "fills": fills,
  2393	        "funding": funding,
  2394	        "extradex_no_anchor": extradex_no_anchor,
  2395	        "inter_anchor_drift": inter,
  2396	    }
  2397	
  2398

    29	    if _idx:
    30	        return
    31	    files = sorted(glob.glob(str(CANDLE_DIR / "20??????.parquet")))
    32	    if not files:
    33	        return
    34	    frames = []
    35	    for f in files:
    36	        df = pd.read_parquet(f, columns=["coin", "timestamp_utc", "close"])
    37	        frames.append(df)
    38	    allc = pd.concat(frames, ignore_index=True)
    39	    allc = allc.dropna(subset=["coin", "timestamp_utc", "close"])
    40	    allc["timestamp_utc"] = allc["timestamp_utc"].astype("int64")
    41	    allc = allc.sort_values(["coin", "timestamp_utc"], kind="mergesort")
    42	    for coin, g in allc.groupby("coin", sort=False):
    43	        _idx[coin] = (g["timestamp_utc"].to_numpy(), g["close"].to_numpy(dtype="float64"))
    44	
    45	
    46	def candle_mark_at(coin: str, ts_ms: int):
    47	    if not _idx:
    48	        _load()
    49	    m = _idx.get(coin)
    50	    if m is None:
    51	        return None
    52	    ts, px = m
    53	    i = bisect_right(ts, ts_ms) - 1
    54	    if i < 0:
    55	        return None
    56	    if ts_ms - int(ts[i]) > MARK_MAX_AGE_MS:
    57	        return None
    58	    return float(px[i])
    59	
    60	
    61	def basis_report(coins: list[str], lo_ms: int, hi_ms: int, step_ms: int = 15 * 60_000):
    62	    """Compare candle_mark_at vs asset_ctxs mark_at across [lo,hi] for `coins`; return per-coin bps basis."""
    63	    sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
    64	    import leadlag_clean_rank_sim as S
    65	    rows = []
    66	    for c in coins:
    67	        diffs = []
    68	        t = lo_ms
    69	        while t <= hi_ms:
    70	            cm = candle_mark_at(c, t)
    71	            am = S.mark_at(c, t)
    72	            if cm and am and am > 0:
    73	                diffs.append((cm - am) / am * 1e4)  # bps
    74	            t += step_ms
    75	        if diffs:
    76	            a = np.array(diffs)
    77	            rows.append({"coin": c, "n": len(a), "med_bps": float(np.median(a)),
    78	                         "mean_abs_bps": float(np.abs(a).mean()), "p95_abs_bps": float(np.percentile(np.abs(a), 95))})
    79	    return pd.DataFrame(rows)
    80	
    81	
    82	if __name__ == "__main__":
    83	    import argparse
    84	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    85	    ap.add_argument("--basis", action="store_true", help="report candle-vs-mark basis on the overlap window")
    86	    ap.add_argument("--coins", default="BTC,ETH,SOL,HYPE,XRP,SUI,ENA,ONDO")
    87	    ap.add_argument("--lo", default="2026-06-24"); ap.add_argument("--hi", default="2026-06-29")
    88	    args = ap.parse_args()
    89	    ms = lambda d: int(pd.Timestamp(d, tz="UTC").timestamp() * 1000)
    90	    _load()
    91	    ncoins = len(_idx)
    92	    cov = [(k, len(v[0])) for k, v in list(_idx.items())[:1]]
    93	    print(f"candle index: {ncoins} coins loaded")
    94	    if args.basis:
    95	        df = basis_report(args.coins.split(","), ms(args.lo), ms(args.hi))
    96	        print(df.to_string(index=False) if len(df) else "no overlap (marks/candles missing in window)")
    97	        if len(df):
    98	            print(f"\nMEDIAN |basis|: {df['mean_abs_bps'].median():.2f} bps  | worst p95: {df['p95_abs_bps'].max():.2f} bps")

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Extract HL EXACT main-dex MARK price from the S3 archive asset_ctxs -> per-coin .npy series.
     3	
     4	Alberto 2026-06-04 "reconcile to the cent": HL computes accountValue on the MARK price, not the last
     5	trade. asset_ctxs (s3://hyperliquid-archive/asset_ctxs/YYYYMMDD.csv.lz4, requester-pays) carries the
     6	EXACT per-minute mark_px for every MAIN-dex coin (~229 coins, 1-min). This is the precise mark M1 must
     7	use for main coins (exotic HIP-3 coins use setOracle -> hyperliquid_oracle, separate).
     8	
     9	STREAMING + CRASH-SAFE (rule 8, mandatory): each day is downloaded, parsed, and FLUSHED to its own
    10	parquet shard (app/data/v15/assetctx_marks_daily/YYYYMMDD.parquet) immediately, then freed. Only ONE
    11	day (~330k rows) is ever in RAM during download. Resumable: existing day-shards are skipped. A separate
    12	bounded `consolidate()` reads the shards and writes per-coin (minute_ms, mark_px) .npy for M1.get_mark.
    13	
    14	Usage:
    15	  python scripts/extract_asset_ctx_marks.py --start 2025-12-01 --end 2026-05-24            # download shards
    16	  python scripts/extract_asset_ctx_marks.py --consolidate                                  # shards -> per-coin npy
    17	  python scripts/extract_asset_ctx_marks.py --start ... --end ... --consolidate            # both
    18	"""
    19	import argparse, csv, io, logging, os, sys, datetime as dt, glob
    20	from pathlib import Path
    21	import boto3, lz4.frame, numpy as np, pandas as pd
    22	
    23	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "research", "v15"))
    24	try:
    25	    from _streaming_io import install_memory_guard
    26	except Exception:
    27	    def install_memory_guard(*a, **k): pass
    28	
    29	logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(message)s", stream=sys.stdout)
    30	log = logging.getLogger("assetctx")
    31	
    32	BUCKET = "hyperliquid-archive"
    33	DAILY = Path("/Users/hermes/quants-lab/app/data/v15/assetctx_marks_daily")
    34	OUT = Path("/Users/hermes/quants-lab/app/data/v15/assetctx_marks")
    35	import urllib.parse as _ulib
    36	
    37	
    38	def download(start: str, end: str):
    39	    """Stream each day's mark_px to its OWN parquet shard, flush, free. Bounded to 1 day RAM. Resumable."""
    40	    DAILY.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    41	    s3 = boto3.client("s3")
    42	    d = dt.datetime.strptime(start, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
    43	    d1 = dt.datetime.strptime(end, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
    44	    while d <= d1:
    45	        shard = DAILY / f"{d.strftime('%Y%m%d')}.parquet"
    46	        if shard.exists():
    47	            log.info(f"  {d}: shard exists, skip"); d += dt.timedelta(days=1); continue
    48	        key = f"asset_ctxs/{d.strftime('%Y%m%d')}.csv.lz4"
    49	        try:
    50	            o = s3.get_object(Bucket=BUCKET, Key=key, RequestPayer="requester")
    51	            data = lz4.frame.decompress(o["Body"].read()).decode("utf-8", "replace")
    52	        except Exception as e:
    53	            log.warning(f"  {key}: {str(e)[:60]} (skip)"); d += dt.timedelta(days=1); continue
    54	        ts_l, coin_l, mk_l = [], [], []
    55	        for r in csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(data)):
    56	            mk = r.get("mark_px")
    57	            if not mk:
    58	                continue
    59	            try:
    60	                ts = int(dt.datetime.strptime(r["time"], "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ").replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc).timestamp() * 1000)
    61	                v = float(mk)
    62	            except (ValueError, TypeError):
    63	                continue
    64	            ts_l.append(ts); coin_l.append(r["coin"]); mk_l.append(v)
    65	        df = pd.DataFrame({"ts_ms": np.asarray(ts_l, dtype="int64"), "coin": coin_l,
    66	                           "mark": np.asarray(mk_l, dtype="float64")})
    67	        tmp = shard.with_suffix(".parquet.tmp")
    68	        df.to_parquet(tmp, index=False)
    69	        tmp.replace(shard)
    70	        log.info(f"  {d} -> {len(df)} rows flushed to {shard.name}")
    71	        del df, ts_l, coin_l, mk_l, data  # free immediately
    72	        d += dt.timedelta(days=1)
    73	
    74	
    75	def consolidate():
    76	    """Per-coin (minute_ms, mark) .npy from day-shards. Bounded: streams shards, one pyarrow column
    77	    read per shard; groups in a dict then writes per coin. Peak ~ full dataset arrays (~900MB-1.3GB),
    78	    under the 12GB guard. (If ever larger: switch to per-coin pass with pyarrow dataset filters.)
    79	
    80	    GUARD (2026-06-11 incident): consolidate REPLACES every npy with exactly the shard set. When the
    81	    shard dir holds only a recent slice (e.g. after an incremental download into a cleaned dir, with
    82	    asset_ctxs publishing ~10d behind), this DESTROYS npy history. Refuse when an existing npy starts
    83	    earlier than the shard set; require the missing day-shards to be re-downloaded first."""
    84	    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    85	    shards = sorted(glob.glob(str(DAILY / "*.parquet")))
    86	    if not shards:
    87	        log.warning("no day-shards to consolidate"); return
    88	    shard_min_day = Path(shards[0]).stem            # YYYYMMDD
    89	    probe = sorted(OUT.glob("*.npy"))
    90	    if probe:
    91	        a = np.load(probe[0], allow_pickle=False)
    92	        if a.shape[1] > 0:
    93	            npy_min_day = dt.datetime.utcfromtimestamp(float(a[0, 0]) / 1000).strftime("%Y%m%d")
    94	            if npy_min_day < shard_min_day:
    95	                log.error(
    96	                    f"REFUSING consolidate: existing npys start {npy_min_day} but shards start "
    97	                    f"{shard_min_day}; consolidating would destroy history. Re-download the missing "
    98	                    f"days into {DAILY} first.")
    99	                sys.exit(2)
   100	    acc: dict[str, list] = {}  # coin -> [df, df, ...] (chronological)
   101	    for sh in shards:
   102	        df = pd.read_parquet(sh, columns=["ts_ms", "coin", "mark"])
   103	        for coin, g in df.groupby("coin"):
   104	            acc.setdefault(coin, []).append(g[["ts_ms", "mark"]].to_numpy())
   105	        del df
   106	    for coin, parts in acc.items():
   107	        m = np.vstack(parts)
   108	        order = np.argsort(m[:, 0].astype("int64"))
   109	        ts_a = m[:, 0].astype("int64")[order]; px_a = m[:, 1].astype("float64")[order]
   110	        arr = np.vstack([ts_a.astype("float64"), px_a])
   111	        p = OUT / f"{_ulib.quote(coin, safe='')}.npy"
   112	        tmp = p.with_name(f"{p.name}.{os.getpid()}.tmp")
   113	        with open(tmp, "wb") as fh:
   114	            np.save(fh, arr)
   115	        tmp.replace(p)
   116	    log.info(f"consolidate: {len(acc)} coins -> {OUT}")
   117	
   118	
   119	def main():
   120	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   121	    ap.add_argument("--start"); ap.add_argument("--end")
   122	    ap.add_argument("--consolidate", action="store_true")
   123	    args = ap.parse_args()
   124	    install_memory_guard(soft_gb=12.0, label="assetctx")
   125	    if args.start and args.end:
   126	        download(args.start, args.end)
   127	    if args.consolidate or not (args.start and args.end):
   128	        consolidate()
   129	    log.info("DONE")
   130	
   131	
   132	if __name__ == "__main__":
   133	    main()

- [research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1304](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1304), [1313](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1313) - near-fetch anchor snapshot can look ahead and double-count positions.
  Failure case: weekly anchor `00:00`, parquet snapshot fetched `12:00`, wallet opens `1 BTC` at `10:00`; `near_fetch=True`, `anchor.positions["BTC"]=1`, no fill `<=00:00`. Seed fabricates `1 BTC` at `00:00`, then the 10:00 fill adds another `1 BTC`, so position becomes `2 BTC` instead of `1`.
  Fix direction: never seed from a snapshot whose timestamp is after the anchor unless replaying/subtracting all fills between anchor and snapshot.

**P2 / Clean Checks**

- Fee/slippage coin-blindness: clean in this file. It uses source `fee` directly in [fill_cash_delta:1056](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1056); no hardcoded `8.64bps` or `fee_rt()` path found.
- `closedPnl`: clean for equity math. It is loaded but not added into cash/PnL, avoiding double-counting.
- Mark asof direction: mostly clean. `searchsorted(..., side="right") - 1` is backward-looking, with staleness caps. The larger mark issue is the missing-anchor-mark event path above, not next-bar asof.
- `--ex-post-seed` is explicitly diagnostic, but if used for selection it is future-leaky by design at [1329-1355](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1329).

**Overall Verdict**

Do not trust m01 output as-is for the selection engine. The default daily series can emit materially wrong equity before first observed carry-in fills, the event bridge can mark corrupted cash snaps as fully markable, and validation can test a different seed mode than the artifact. It is poisoned until the P0s and the P1 validation/data-loss issues are fixed or downstream is proven to hard-exclude every affected row/wallet.

**P1**

- [research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1895](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1895), [1965](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1965) - validation cross-checks use `compute_eq_at()` default `causal_seed=False`, while emitted rows default to causal seed.
  Failure case: the carry-in case above emits wrong Dec 8 equity, but validation at fetch can use future-backed ex-post seeding and look accurate. The audit table can certify a different reconstruction than the artifact.
  Fix direction: pass the emitted `causal_seed` into `today_crosscheck` and `position_validation`, or report them as separate non-selection diagnostics.

- [research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:905](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:905), [909](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:909), [939](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:939), [943](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:943) - funding/ledger JSON load failures are silently ignored.
  Failure case: wallet has a `withdraw usdc=5000` ledger JSON file, but it is truncated/corrupt. Loader catches and continues; equity remains overstated by `$5000`, `unknown_ledger_types=[]`, no row-level incomplete flag.
  Fix direction: warn and mark wallet/audit as data incomplete or hard fail the wallet.

- [research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:857](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:857), [865](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:865) - by-wallet fill parquet failure is swallowed before fallback.
  Failure case: by-wallet file exists but is missing `price`; daily files are unavailable or stale. Fast path raises, `fills=[]`, fallback finds nothing, wallet reconstructs from anchors/ledger with no trades.
  Fix direction: log the fast-path exception and fail if both fast path and fallback cannot prove complete coverage.

- [research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1570](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1570), [1606](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1606) - first partial week is dropped if `--start` is between weekly anchors.
  Failure case: request `--start 2025-12-03`, previous anchor `2025-12-01`, next anchor `2025-12-08`. Rows for Dec 3-7 are skipped because pre-start anchor is excluded.
  Fix direction: load events from previous anchor, or explicitly report the truncated date range.

- [research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:860](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:860), [883](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:883) - input I/O is not truly streaming.
  Failure case: a large by-wallet parquet or daily fill parquet is read fully into RAM before filtering. Memory guard can kill or exception a whale wallet, causing survivorship in produced series.
  Fix direction: use pyarrow dataset filters/row-group streaming and project/filter before materializing.

**P2 / Clean Checks**

- Fee/slippage coin-blindness: clean in this file. It uses source `fee` directly in [fill_cash_delta:1056](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1056); no hardcoded `8.64bps` or `fee_rt()` path found.
- `closedPnl`: clean for equity math. It is loaded but not added into cash/PnL, avoiding double-counting.
- Mark asof direction: mostly clean. `searchsorted(..., side="right") - 1` is backward-looking, with staleness caps. The larger mark issue is the missing-anchor-mark event path above, not next-bar asof.
- `--ex-post-seed` is explicitly diagnostic, but if used for selection it is future-leaky by design at [1329-1355](/Users/hermes/quants-lab/research/v15/v15_m01_equity_reconstruct.py:1329).

**Overall Verdict**

Do not trust m01 output as-is for the selection engine. The default daily series can emit materially wrong equity before first observed carry-in fills, the event bridge can mark corrupted cash snaps as fully markable, and validation can test a different seed mode than the artifact. It is poisoned until the P0s and the P1 validation/data-loss issues are fixed or downstream is proven to hard-exclude every affected row/wallet.
